Chapter 3051: Tomb Guardian

“Whoosh!”

The appearance of the Red Dragon Swallowing the Sun emblem made the black ball move faster. Thousands of black crystal threads that looked like nerves suddenly popped up and crawled into the armor of the ‘Gold Sun’.

The ‘Gold Sun’ had employed the translucent, seamless technology of refining. There were not even obvious gaps in the joints that needed to be rotated flexibly. However, the moment the black crystal threads touched its surface, they vanished into thin air like a stream flowing into an ocean. From the depths of the ‘Gold Sun’s’ spiritual residence, a soft noise echoed. After a ‘Chi’ sound, a black hole appeared around the breastplate, from which a lot of bright gold spiritual flames spurted out. The breastplate slightly collapsed before it was opened up.

Li Yao’s heart was beating so fast that it seemed that blood was going to spurt out from his chest to his brain and then his eyeballs.

The ‘Gold Sun’s’ spiritual residence was empty. There were no drivers, either. But after scanning carefully, he discovered that a small corpse was sitting cross-legged in the small space.

The dead body seemed to have been stored in the Spiritual Nexus for thousands of years. The dried flesh and blood seemed to be made of metal and were emitting bright gold brilliance. There was no sense of hideousness or horror at all. Instead, it was as magnificent and dominating as a gold statue.

Its body size was much smaller than that of an ordinary person when sitting down. There was no telling whether it was born like this, or the water inside its body had completely evaporated after death, causing it to shrink.

There were no traces of armor or magical equipment on its body. The only thing that could be found was a Cosmos Ring on its finger. The Red Dragon Swallowing the Sun emblem was carved on the surface of the ring, too. However, nineteen iron nails that were thicker than fingers had been nailed into the top of its head. Every iron nail had pierced through the skull and the brain. The tips of the iron nails congregated in the depths of its

“Soul Suppressing Nail?”

Li Yao was secretly surprised. He could tell from the runes on the hat that it was a piece of magical equipment that was rarely-seen even in the era of the ancient Cultivators. It was mainly used to seal the soul so that the Cultivators with strong mental power would not turn into ghosts after they were killed. Some of them could even seal the soul in the rotten body and endure the excruciating pain that was worse than death.

However, there were no traces of fighting inside the ‘Golden Sun’. Li Yao did not believe that there was anything that could drive the Soul Stabilizer into the brain of the user of the ‘Golden Sun’.

He observed the posture of the body. Although he was in great pain, he was sitting cross-legged with his heart facing the sky, as if he had passed away.

Could it be that the nineteen Soul Suppressing Nails were all injected into his brain by himself… in order to seal his own soul and wait for a chance of survival?

Li Yao shuddered and was on full alert.

Of course, if the enemy was really trying to find a chance of survival from him, he wouldn’t be scared at all. Mental competitions, possession, and anti-possession were exactly what he was best at!

Besides, his instincts told him that the golden corpse in front of him had lost all vitality. Not even a cell or a telepathic thought could pose any threat to him.

It was quite odd.

If the enemy had endured the inhuman pain in order to protect his soul, how could his soul have perished in only several thousand years?

It must be noted that Gao Xingce, a captain of an ordinary starship from the Star Ocean Imperium and the captain of ‘Sparkle’, whom Li Yao had met in the depths of the sea of mithril below Boneyard, had been able to strip away his consciousness and memories layer by layer and preserve his remaining soul for almost ten thousand years.

The helmsman of ‘Gold Sun’ or ‘Armageddon’ could not be weaker than the captain of ‘Sparkle’, could he?

Li Yao looked at the black ball in confusion.

He needed Little Black to tell him the answer.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

The black crystal threads that extended out of the black ball all swam to the head of the bright gold corpse and tied up the Soul Suppressing Nail. All the information that Little Black collected from the outside world, including Li Yao’s rise from the magical equipment graveyard and his rise to become the best expert in the Pangu Universe, flooded into the brain that had already dried up and evaporated. The golden corpse did not move at all. Li Yao still could not sense the slightest hint of life inside it. But the brilliance on the surface of the black ball was even more brilliant and intense. Very soon, another black crystal thread extended out toward Li Yao.

It seemed to be a kind invitation.

At least the enemy did not invade his brain by force and tried to swallow his spirit and will.

Li Yao pondered for a moment. Of course, he could see the risks, but he didn’t want to doubt Little Black’s kindness. Besides, the beast tide outside had already turned the world upside down. The mysterious ‘heavenly tower’ might be hiding a world-destroying crisis, too. Li Yao was in desperate need of an answer from the ‘Gold Sun’ or the master of the apocalypse, or at least a solution.

Li Yao made up his mind and ignored Lu Qingchen’s shout. He extended the strongest telepathic thought from the center of his eyebrow and connected it with the black crystal threads.

BOOM!

A lightning seemed to have struck deep inside Li Yao’s brain.

With the black ball as the medium, the mental bridge between him and the bright gold corpse was completed.

In a trance, the world around him blurred and twisted. Countless shadows blinked and formed overlapping trajectories, as if everything that had happened in the ‘museum of prehistoric civilization’ over the past ten thousand years had happened again within one second.

Even with Li Yao’s god-level Cultivation and the weird soul from Earth, he couldn’t help but exclaim because of the huge amount of information.

Then, he ‘saw’ that the bright gold corpse in front of him slowly opened its eyes!

It was an extremely weird feeling.

There was not the slightest hint of life in the bright gold corpse, but the depths of its dry eyes were beaming with what could be called ‘human emotions’. Colorful colors were flashing one after another. Li Yao could clearly sense the boundless dominance, the extremely furious brutality, the wisdom that was as deep as the ocean… The twenty distinct and different human emotions, and even the confusion when faced with the boundless ocean.

As streams of information flooded in, Li Yao vaguely understood what was going on.

The owner of the bright gold body was indeed dead. Maybe he had been dead for thousands of years.

Before his death, he had refined his body into a ‘warehouse’ and stored countless mental imprints inside as his legacy-a database that was equivalent to his flesh and blood.

However, his mental imprint was too intense and gave people a ‘realistic’ feeling, as if the person in the picture would break away from the 3D light beam and turn into a real entity after he took a picture.

Even the liquid crystal processor, which had stored a lot of his mental imprints, had been contaminated by his scent over the long years. The fact that he could kill an Armored Storm Dragon with one strike was indicative of his invincibility when he was alive!

Looking at his opponent’s eyes that were brimming with brilliance, Li Yao was extremely excited. He threw out the question that everyone would ask in such a situation. “Are you… the Supreme Emperor?”

The bright gold corpse was silent for a long time.

Of course, the dead body could not talk, but the mental imprint stored deep inside the dry cells was quickly analyzing the identity of the questioner, the current situation, and the outside world. After high-speed calculations, it gave the most appropriate response. “The erosion of time is enough to destroy everything. The once-glorious imperium has long been reduced to ruins. Even the kingdoms that were rebuilt on the ruins have been burnt to ashes. The so-called ‘Supreme Emperor’ is nothing but an absurd story. It is just a myth of comfort for the people who are shivering in the darkness and blood in the collapsing universe.

The ripples were transmitted to Li Yao’s brain through the black crystal wires, as if the bright gold corpse was talking.

“Besides, in the mysterious primeval relics, the unparalleled experts who once dominated the Pangu Universe and the rulers of the peak civilizations over the past billions of years are everywhere. Thousands of deities in every corner of the universe have turned into dry bones, weeds, bushes, and even fog in this place. Even the ’emperor’ of the human civilization is just one of the moths that are rushing to the fire.

The bright gold corpse continued. “Too much time has passed. Countless pieces of information have been lost. Your Supreme Emperor has been obliterated just like all the other gods. I am just his shadow, his message his brand, and the guardian of the primeval relics or the ‘civilization tomb’.

“Gravekeeper, gravekeeper,” Li Yao had no time to care about the identity of the other party. He clenched his fists and said urgently, “Tell me, what is going on? What is the truth of the primeval relics? Why did so many elites of the ancient civilization perish in this place one after another? Even countless ancient civilizations were wiped out mercilessly. Also, what’s the situation with the ferocious beasts outside? Why are the primeval civilizations attacking each other? Why have they turned into such hideous and terrifying ferocious beasts? What are they fighting for?”

“And you, or rather, your master, the real ‘Supreme Emperor’. Where did he come from? Who is he? What is he looking for in the primeval relics? Why is his Giant God Soldier ‘Gold Sun’ and the Blood God’s Giant God Soldier ‘Apocalyptic Calamity’ here?

“Our civilization, and yours, is in peril. I need answers to everything!

“There is no answer.”

The gravekeeper was silent for a long time. Then he replied to Li Yao’s brain through the black crystal threads, “Or rather, you are the answer. The Supreme Emperor left the laboratory and the legacy of tremendous information before his death because he hoped that the latecomers could follow his path and find the ultimate answer to the riddle that he failed to solve until his death… Who are we exactly?”

Chapter 3052: Legends Of The Supreme Emperor

“Am I the answer?” Li Yao mumbled to himself, “Does the ‘Supreme Emperor’ not know who he is, where he is from, and where he is born?”

“Yes. The man who was known as the ‘Supreme Emperor’ in the legends later and left countless bizarre and absurd legends was just a passer-by who lost his way in the sea of stars. He was a man who spent his entire life searching for answers. It’s a pity that, until the end of his life, he did not know the significance of the creation of his civilization and the entire human civilization, nor did he know the mission that we… might have delayed for billions of years. The gravekeeper’s voice was filled with desolation, antiquity, and helplessness. As the information streams rippled and surged, the vague illusions nearby became clearer.

It was like going back hundreds of thousands of years to when the Pangu Civilization had just discovered the primeval relics and established a large, cutting-edge laboratory in this place. “Supreme Emperor… My memory begins with a tiny embryo.

The gravekeeper’s voice turned lower and lower, like the ravings in a dream. He had entered the role of the Supreme Emperor, or rather, he was playing the Supreme Emperor’s last words. “I was like an ancient fungus floating in the primitive ocean, drifting in the hot and nutritious seawater. In a few days, I experienced the path that would take billions of years from a bacteria to a carbon-based intelligent life. It was not until then that I realized that I was not floating in the ocean but living in an artificial nutrient solution.

“It was indeed a weird feeling.

“If I were really a bacterium, or just a cell, or even an embryo of a carbon-based intelligent life, I should not have developed self-awareness and precise thinking ability. I should not have been able to ‘awareness’ my own living environment, and I should not have been able to release things like ‘telepathic thoughts’ to perceive and explore the world around me.

“But as it happened, I had the ability to clearly perceive the entire process of me evolving from a small bacterium waving flagella and tentacles into a multi-cellular organism or even an intelligent life embryo. I could even scan the strengthened glass wall outside the nutrition liquids and the vast and complicated laboratory outside the culture chamber.

“It was a laboratory that was hundreds of times larger and more advanced than the one in front of your eyes. Thousands of similar culture chambers seemed to be breeding batches of fetuses or similar things at the same time. Many fetuses that had grown mature but were not very successful were exploited by the waste, too. They were dissected and studied in the operating tables and crystal processors of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan for virtual experiments. Weird, whimsical experiments were conducted day and night. As the gravekeeper played the last words of the ‘Supreme Emperor’, the environment around them changed, too. Countless Pangu Clan and Nuwa Clan members in sealed, sterile clothes were indeed engaged in experiments.

On the operating table and on the light beam of the crystal processor, there were all kinds of bizarre-shaped animals, but most of them were still recognizable as human beings.

“At that time, I didn’t understand what the primeval species were doing at all. I only knew that they raised thousands of embryos that were similar to me and stimulated the embryos in various ways, hoping to activate the mysterious power buried deep inside the embryos. But most of the experiments failed.

The Supreme Emperor continued speaking in a low and sad voice. “Some of the fetuses were unable to activate any strength and came to an abrupt end in the middle of their evolution, turning into all kinds of carbon-based creatures without the slightest bit of intelligence, animals that feast on raw meat and blood. Some of the fetuses could not withstand the intense stimulation and exploded and were destroyed, melting into the nutrition liquids. Most of the fetuses, although successfully growing into fetuses and turning from fetuses into babies, did not show any extraordinary abilities after they fell to the ground. Except for their reproductive ability and adaptive ability, they were far weaker than the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, the Gonggong Clan, the Zhurong Clan, and the other advanced and powerful prehistoric species.

“The researchers of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan seem to be very dissatisfied with the experiment results. I can sense their anxiety, anger, and even desperation.

“At that time, I was just a tiny embryo that was no more complicated than a bacterium. To my knowledge, the primeval species were more than gods and devils; they were creatures of a higher dimension. There was something that could make them feel ‘anxious’ and ‘desperate’. I could not imagine what it was exactly. “All in all, the researchers of the primeval civilization conducted experiments on countless fetuses and inputted the data of the failed experiments into the next batch of fetuses. The parameters of the experiment environment and the stimulation were changed, and the next batch of fetuses suffered another failure. However, in every batch of fetuses that failed, 1% of the fetuses were lucky enough to survive. They were not destroyed by the intense stimulation, nor did they embark on the wrong path of evolution. They grew and matured in a relatively weak way. They merely maintained the form of fetuses, absorbed the fetuses quietly, and continued hibernating.

“I was one of the luckiest embryos. It took me three years to go from cells to fetuses.

A culture chamber that looked like a crystal tower appeared in front of Li Yao’s eyes. At first, the chamber seemed to be empty except for the bright green nutrition liquids.

Only by releasing his telepathic thoughts to the maximum could Li Yao detect a cell-like object in the nutrition liquids.

The reason why it was called a ‘similar cell’ was because the structure of an ordinary cell should be very simple, but the inside of this cell was extremely complicated, as if… it contained the entire universe.

As time went by, the researchers around the crystal tower changed one batch after another.

From the Pangu Clan, which was tall and had a deformed brain in the beginning, to the Nuwa Clan, which had a human head and a snake body and a tail that boasted ‘gene capsules’ that could disassemble and combine genes at will, to the Kuafu Clan of plants and the Houyi Clan of insects, as well as the Zhurong Clan and the Gonggong Clan, and so on, every prehistoric, carbon-based intelligent life had been refining the embryo with their unique secret arts and injecting tremendous nutrition, gene segments, spiritual waves, and information into the embryo.

Under the stimulation and ‘feeding’, the embryo slowly expanded. From the bacteria in the beginning, it grew into a bright gold meatball more than twenty centimeters in diameter, like a crystal that had shrunk the star billions of times. It revolved slowly and breathed in and out, emitting mysterious, glamorous brilliance. As the Supreme Emperor had said, it had been hibernating and absorbing for three years and was unwilling to break out of the shell one second earlier.

“I seem to be the experiment subject who lasted the longest in the embryo state in the entire laboratory.

The Supreme Emperor said, “After I distinguished myself among the countless embryos, naturally, I attracted the attention of most of the researchers in the primeval era. All the resources of the laboratory were focused on me, and I was stimulated more and more. Under such stimulation, I saw a lot of pictures and awakened a lot of knowledge, wisdom that did not belong to this era or the universe. “It was as if this was not my first life but my ‘reincarnation’. Before I was trained in the laboratory, I had lived once, or even countless times, in the boundless sea of stars beyond the Pangu Universe. I struggled, resisted, collapsed, despaired, and fled!

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

“Countless pictures were flashing inside my brain. Even the simplest picture was beyond description.

“I saw myself in the past, or human beings similar to us, with bright gold stripes all over our bodies. We were born with the ability to summon the wind and summon the rain, and we were born with the ability to steer all creatures. We stepped on unbelievably enormous warships, waved lightning and thunder, and roamed the sea of stars and even the multiverse. We conquered the intelligent creatures in different forms from us, deprived them of their wisdom, devoured their civilization, and melted the essence of their civilization into ours. We even transformed everything about their civilization into information streams and carved them into our gene pool. “Whether it is the giants that are as tall as mountains on the super-low-density planets in the center of the glorious sea of stars, the crystal-clear, weak, boneless oceanic people on the oceanic planets, or the solar-powered insects that are born on the planets that are too close to the stars and barely have an atmosphere, or the extremely hot planets… All kinds of intelligent creatures that look hideous and won’t even appear in our nightmares have been conquered by us. They have become tiny and clear marks in the gene bank of mankind.

“Every one of us, every cell in the body of every human being, is a ‘museum of cosmic civilization’. Everything that contains hundreds of carbon-based intelligent creatures is stored on every gene strand. They are our trophies.

“With dragons as symbols, we can even unlock our abilities and turn ourselves into real dragons. The so-called ‘dragons’ are super creatures that swallow billions of creatures and build themselves up with the genetic essence of billions of creatures.

“However…

“Even the super creatures as strong as dragons or gods and devils will reach their limits when they are faced with the mysterious, boundless universe.

“After our civilization climbed to the peak, it hit rock bottom almost instantly. The golden years of invincibility were shattered into pieces. We turned from the dominators of the multiverse into stray dogs. The glory and glory of victory turned into endless humiliation and fear.

“Who are our enemies exactly? How did we end up like this? If this is everything I experienced in my ‘previous life’, then how did I come from my ‘previous life’ to ‘the present world’?

“I would very much like to know the answers to these questions. I would very much like to continue exploring the bizarre scenes and the endless past.

“But the primeval researchers in the laboratory were not willing to let me continue the intense stimulation. I could vaguely sense that they were… scared of the awakening of human beings.

Chapter 3053: Dragon God’S Clone

The Supreme Emperor’s last words employed a very advanced method of information transmission and interaction, allowing Li Yao to experience everything he felt in the pasteven everything he felt when he was in the embryo state.

It was a natural-born picture that had been embedded in the segments of the Supreme Emperor Gene billions of years ago. Li Yao seemed to have seen everything, too. Or rather, the emperor’s last words had triggered the mysteries buried deep inside his gene pool.

In the blink of an eye, an incomparably dazzling sea of stars appeared in front of Li Yao’s eyes. Each and every one of them was resplendent and magnificent. They were incomparably large. Whether it was the stable state of the three-dimensional space or the super storm of the four-dimensional space, it was as if they were walking on flat ground. On these giant tungsten warships stood countless humans-not the ordinary humans of today, but rather the “True Human Beings” whose bodies were surrounded by electric arcs and energy networks. Their skin flickered with golden streaks and specks of silver brilliance.

Li Yao saw the human beings billions of years ago passing through the multiverse and studying the mysteries of the white dwarf and the neutron star, the black holes and the wormholes, and even the four-dimensional space. He saw them conquering bizarre civilizations one after another. Countless civilizations that looked like gods and devils were shivering in fear under the sabers of human beings and offering everything they had

-technology, wisdom, the form of social tissues, and even the gene map-all of which turned into complicated information streams that were then compressed and encrypted into the gene strands of human beings.

It meant that the civilization of mankind had turned into a dragon and swallowed the carbon-based civilizations one after another, becoming the ‘king of kings’ of the multiverse. As the gene strands grew longer and longer, the glory of mankind finally spread to almost every corner of the multiverse.

However,

The decline of prosperity was inevitable in history. However brilliant a civilization was created by mankind, it was inevitable that it would decline one day.

Presumably, the civilization of mankind billions of years ago encountered a ‘flood’ when it was at the peak of its power. It was utterly defeated by the flood and became a stray dog.

The history was unclear in the Supreme Emperor’s gene strands and had not been analyzed yet. Therefore, Li Yao could only see the broken, rusted fleets fleeing in panic in the sea of stars, only to be destroyed one by one.

Even so, the picture in front of Li Yao’s eyes alone was enough to set off ten thousand thunderclaps in the depths of Li Yao’s braincells. Every gene strand was ready to burst out.

“Such a splendid, marvelous, and unbelievable civilization of the primeval human beings! What—what is going on exactly?”.

Li Yao shouted crazily in his heart, As it turns out, the Supreme Emperor was not the hope to find the depths of the ancient relics when he was dying. He was born in this place in the first place. Maybe one of the laboratories inside the tower was his ‘home’!

W

“However, why is it like this? Even if the Pangu civilization wanted to produce human beings, it would have taken at most ten months for human beings to grow from life seeds to fetuses to croaks. Why did the Supreme Emperor give birth to them for three years and grow them into… such weird golden meatballs?

“Besides, he is still in the embryo state, yet he has awakened so many ancient memories and even grasped self-awareness and clear thinking that are comparable to that of an adult. That’s too unbelievable!

Li Yao thought quickly and thought of a possibility.

“Right. Just like Long Yangjun.

“Long Yangjun was a human warrior from the primeval era hundreds of thousands of years ago. But her genes and life seeds were extracted and condensed into an embryo. After hibernating in an unbelievable way for hundreds of thousands of years, she was born again.

“It must be noted that, when Long Yangjun was born more than a hundred years ago, the Nuwa warship was already empty. It was all thanks to the automated magical equipment that she was able to complete her modification. It is evident that the technology of gene reproduction and automation was very mature at that time, and it was very likely that it was passed on from the primeval relics. “Therefore, is it possible that the Supreme Emperor’s genes originated from the maker of the black wall billions of years ago? “Yes, that must be it!

“Is there any better way to unveil the mysteries of the primeval era and break the black wall that sealed the Pangu Universe than to ‘clone’a real maker of the black wall?

“The experts and scholars of the Pangu Civilization must’ve collected the genes of the makers of the black wall from the depths of the primeval relics. Maybe it was their dead bodies, maybe it was the blood that was sealed in certain magical equipment, or maybe it was the legacy left by the makers of the black wall.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

“The technology of the modern human civilization is so advanced that two life seeds are enough to breed the next generation automatically. Since the Nuwa Clan in the primeval civilization was a natural-born expert in gene modification, a drop of the blood of the black wall maker is enough to replicate a brand-new one, which is not beyond the limits of technology.

“According to the description of the Supreme Emperor, the maker of the black wall seems to boast the ability to compress and embed the genes of countless carbon-based intelligent creatures in the universe into his own gene strands. Therefore, the Supreme Emperor’s embryo contains the information of countless super civilizations in the primeval era, including the creator of the black wall. It is not surprising that he has such sharp thinking and perception before he is fully born!

“As for the Pangu civilization being scared of his awakening… It was perfectly normal.

“Assuming that the civilization of mankind today wants to replicate a living member of the Pangu Clan or the Nuwa Clan, they must be very cautious and full of expectation, too.

Li Yao calmed himself down and continued reading the Supreme Emperor’s last words. “They… the ancient, carbon-based intelligent creatures were scared of my awakening, looked forward to my awakening, desired to make use of my abilities, and feared my abilities.

The gravekeeper continued, “They tried everything they could to stimulate me to unlock the unknown gene strands, hoping to fully analyze the secrets hidden in my gene strands. But they also refined all kinds of… things that could be called ‘marks’. They tried to seal and control me from both the genetic and the mental levels. They even tried to implant ‘bombs’ into my gene strands so that they could destroy me with the ‘genetic bombs’ when necessary.

“Of course, that was all in the future. I didn’t realize it until a long, long time later when I returned to this place and observed everything.

“At that time, I was just an ignorant embryo after all. I was not even an intelligent life in the truest sense of the word. I treated everything they imposed on me as an interesting game and had a great time playing it.

“Just like that, I grew up slowly. I grew bigger and bigger, and I had stronger and stronger abilities. When I was still sealed in the crystal tower, I could manipulate everything around me, including everything and… everyone.

An unbelievable picture appeared in front of Li Yao’s eyes again.

In the spacious laboratory, with the crystal tower where the embryo of the Supreme Emperor was stored as the center, all the experimental magical equipment and equipment nearby flew into the air unsteadily, as if they were dancing and colliding with invisible hands.

In the second picture, as the bright golden ball of meat bobbed up and down in the nutrition liquids, all the primeval crystal processors nearby were turned on and running at a high speed automatically. Three-dimensional light beams were projected, which were full of glamorous patterns that looked like the traces of the fleet of the ancient human beings when they passed through the four-dimensional space unstoppably.

In the third picture, the experiment equipment and the crystal processor were both safe and sound, but the dozens of researchers who were doing the research seemed to have been hypnotized. They stared at the Supreme Emperor’s embryo and mumbled to themselves. Their legs were shivering, as if they had lost their soul because of the suppression of the baby.

“My ability is getting stronger, and my consciousness and thinking are getting clearer. I can even communicate with the researchers who have the keenest senses.

The gravekeeper continued telling the story of the Supreme Emperor. “I even have a… friend who is the daughter of the supervisor of the laboratory. She is a minor member of the Nuwa Clan, and she is also a talented and excellent expert in genetic research.

A new picture appeared in front of Li Yao’s eyes.

In the messy laboratory, a young-looking Nuwa, who was only three to four meters tall, was crouching in front of the crystal tower where the embryo of the Supreme Emperor was sealed. She looked at the golden ball inside the crystal tower silently. There was not the slightest hint of the ‘dignity’ or ‘sacredness’ on her face. Instead, she looked like a human girl who was sometimes happy, sometimes innocent, sometimes sad, sometimes smiling, and sometimes excited.

“I learned a lot of things from my… friend and have a preliminary understanding about the universe we are in right now.

The gravekeeper’s tone suddenly turned gentler. But after the gentleness, it was even more frustrated. “At the very least, I know that I am not the only one who has been sealed. My friend and her compatriots have been sealed in the tiny ‘ancient relics’ far away from their real hometown, too. They’ve been fighting for their sacred mission for countless years, but the hope of them finding the place is still slim. There is no telling how long they will stay in this place. “My friend grew up in this place and the City of Silver. She never left the ancient relics and returned to her real hometown.

“I asked her why, but she only told me three words-isolation zone.

“I also want to exchange more information with her. What is the ‘isolation zone’? What is their mission? What are the pictures that keep flashing in the depths of my sea of consciousness? Can I be born with real hands, feet, and mobility like her? Why are the ‘adults’ other than her so scared of me? If they are scared of me, why did they create me and stimulate me?

“I had a lot of questions that I wanted to ask her, but her father, as well as the supervisor of the laboratory and the ‘manufacturing project’, did not like it when his daughter was alone with the weird meatball that was me. She was forced to leave the project when her father found out that she snuck into the laboratory during her non-research time. The ‘sealing’ and ‘punishment’ against me intensified, too.

Chapter 3054: The Truth Of The Battle Of The Ruins

In the picture that constituted the last words of the Supreme Emperor, the innocent girl of the Nuwa Clan had been replaced by the indifferent, hostile adult researchers. There was a thick ice shell on the faces of the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and other intelligent species, as if all their feelings were hidden behind the thick ice.

Li Yao finally realized something very strange.

As far as he could see, more than 70% of the feelings of the prehistoric species who established laboratories in the primeval relics had been sealed.

Maybe, only such people could withstand the endless, bizarre, unbelievable mysteries and fears in the middle level of the primeval relics.

If they had normal emotions and realized that hundreds of species that were as strong as the Pangu Civilization over billions of years had perished, they would’ve gone mad and failed to make any progress in their research.

Only the girl, perhaps because of her young age, was able to show some normal emotions. It was probably because she was particularly close to the Supreme Emperor.

Then, the picture blinked. The researchers were performing all kinds of intense and destructive experiments on the golden meat ball.

The researchers stimulated the defense ability of the golden ball with intense electric arcs, tested the self-recovery ability of the golden ball with highly toxic and corrosive liquids, and even intentionally denied the golden ball any nutrition for a long time to test its limits.

It was as if they did not want the Supreme Emperor in the golden meat ball to be born at all.

Such vigilant, hostile, and even malicious treatment naturally aroused the Supreme Emperor’s resistance. In the next picture, a tall member of the Pangu Clan was lying on the ground outside the crystal tower. His head had exploded, and blood and cooling liquids were spluttering everywhere.

Shocked researchers were all around him. Even though they had sealed most of their emotions, they could not stop the uncontrollable fear they had for the golden ball. “I killed a researcher.

The tomb watcher’s voice was both dull and cold. He said, one word after another, “The guy was a geneticist and a pharmacologist. He was best at cracking the fragments deep inside the gene strands with various naturally-extracted and synthesized poisons. He injected almost a hundred kinds of venom into my embryo to test my self-recovery and immunity. He even tried to implant a seal into my gene strand, which was a gene fragment that was full of his commands. When I was really born, if necessary, my body would automatically secrete all kinds of hormones with a telepathic thought, which would be synthesized into a deadly poison and poison myself.

“This is one of the seals that the primeval civilization came up with to contain me and all mankind.

“It was not the most effective seal, but it was indeed the most painful one. I was almost poisoned to death by his drugs many times, only to be saved by his antidotes. In a sense, my brain was indeed full of bizarre illusions, as if the past billions of years ago were flashing and being stolen by him one by one.

“In most cases, I don’t hate what the researchers did. After all, most of the experiments caused me little pain. Besides, I could read the secrets deep inside the genes through the experiments and get to know myself better. “But the methods of the researcher were too painful. I didn’t hate him. I just wanted to get rid of the miserable state. Also, now that I was separated from my only friend, I was filled with frustration and frustration for the first time and simply wanted to vent it out. “Therefore, when he walked toward me with a syringe filled with venom again, I concentrated all my strength and performed a small ‘push’ in his brain.

“It’s very difficult to explain what the so-called ‘push’ is. It’s like an upgraded version of hypnosis. With a sweep of my telepathic thoughts, I will be able to clearly scan the way his brain functions. I will even be able to determine the specific part of his brain that can influence his decisions. Then, as long as I slightly compress his mind, I will be able to influence his decisions.

“I hoped, no, commanded him to inject the venom into his body.

“To be fair, I did not intend to kill him, because I knew that he would prepare antidotes at the same time when he was preparing a venom. After all, they only wanted to stimulate my genetic memory with the venom and test my self-recovery and immunity at the same time. They did not mean to kill me. Although the antidotes in the past had all kinds of side effects and the process was quite painful, they all worked wonders when it came to reviving the dead.

“Therefore, I believe that, even if he injects venom into himself, he can save himself with the antidote immediately. At most, he will suffer a little bit.

“However, my ‘push’ shocked the researcher of the Pangu Clan. He summoned all his brainwaves and mental power to resist me subconsciously.

“For me, this level of mental confrontation is just a game in the wind and rain. I didn’t care and increased the ‘push’ force, but the guy’s eyes were wide open. Every inch of his skin was red, and his face was like a cracked ice shell. He couldn’t have been more terrified. The wrinkles on the back of his head were spurting hot streams. In the end, his head exploded before the other researchers realized what was going on.

“I made a big mistake. “Or rather, I have shown too much capability and terrifying potential. It is no longer something that the Pangu civilization can suppress with regular methods. They have decided to suspend the research project and put me in a super long hibernation state for thousands of years, tens of thousands of years, hundreds of thousands of years… or forever, until they make progress in other fields and are confident that they can control me and unfreeze me for new studies.

“I didn’t have the slightest chance to resist.

“At that time, I was just a small embryo after all. No matter how many mysteries were buried in the depths of my genes, I was still unable to do anything due to the lack of nutrition, energy, and a body of flesh and blood. It was one thing to hypnotize an unsuspecting researcher, but it was a whole different thing to hypnotize over a hundred mental combat experts who were on high alert and had all kinds of ‘brainwave amplifiers’. The latter was a hundred times more difficult.

“I knew that they had injected the hibernation drugs and all kinds of weird nutrition drugs into my body and turned the crystal tower that carried me into a giant, transparent amber, but there was nothing I could do.

“The vigorous and turbulent sea of consciousness gradually calmed down and solidified.

“I can sense the range and routes around me. They are shrinking and dispersing, too.

“Before, I could sense dozens of kinds of visible lights, invisible lights, and high-frequency electromagnetic waves in the hundreds of square meters nearby. I could hear the heartbeats of countless researchers, the flow of their blood, and the sizzling sound of their brainwaves. But the glamorous brilliance and noises were all taken away. Sleepiness surged up uncontrollably like a thick black tide and covered the golden meat ball in a black membrane.

“I should’ve fallen asleep like this. Maybe, I will never wake up again.

“But right then, deafening noises and earthshaking explosions echoed outside the laboratory. Even I, who was about to hibernate, could feel the intense scent of killing.

“Now that I’ve been injected with a lot of hibernation drugs and completed 95% of the hibernation procedure, I don’t think there will be any more trouble. All the researchers left me alone and ran out.

“I don’t know how long it took, but my friend rushed in and rushed to the crystal tower that sealed me.

“Her body was smeared with blood and smoke. She seemed to be in a panic.

“She told me that all the researchers of the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and the other species living in the primeval relics were betrayed by the outsiders.

“Just as they feared the ‘True Human Beings’ of the primeval era, the Pangu Clan outside feared them, too. They feared the ancient mysteries that they studied day and night. Therefore, they grasped extraordinary technology and ancient magical equipment. The clansmen outside demanded that they hand over all the research files and the war machines that they excavated. They even asked the clansmen outside to teach them how to use war machines such as the ‘space knights’.

“However, those are just excuses. “My friend told me that, outside the primeval relics, her compatriots had been engaged in a protracted civil war that almost destroyed the entire Pangu civilization. It also destroyed the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, the Zhurong Clan, the Gonggong Clan, the Kuafu Clan, the Houyi Clan… All the beautiful feelings and moral principles deep inside the hearts of the ancient carbon-based intelligent species.

“Even though both sides were at the end of their rope in the civil war, like two vipers that were biting each other’s ankles, they would rather perish together than to let go of each other.

“The desperate and desperate parties both focused their attention on the ancient relics, hoping to recruit the experts and researchers there as their ‘secret weapons’. At the same time, they were scared that the innocent and gullible non-combatants would be lured to the enemy’s side. If so, they would rather make the first move and destroy all the experts and researchers together with their research results!

“It sounds unbelievable, but a war is the most unreasonable thing in the world. The two parties who are on a killing spree have no scruples at all. They call themselves the real dominators and the rightful successors of the Pangu Civilization, which means that they have the right to command their compatriots in the primeval relics. If the experts, scholars, and researchers in the primeval relics do not listen to their commands, they will be traitors of the entire civilization. Naturally, they will be punished without mercy!

Chapter 3055: Spacetime Arc

There was infinite sorrow in the Supreme Emperor’s voice.

Li Yao sighed, too.

The stronger a civilization was, the more arrogant it would be, and the more likely it would destroy itself. Maybe, the hundreds of ancient civilizations before the Pangu civilization were not destroyed by the primeval relics but by their own ambitions that were triggered by the primeval relics.

Li Yao sighed and listened attentively.

“My only friend told me that it was the best warriors and the cruelest killing machines of the Pangu Civilization who attacked the primeval relics. Also, they were controlled by a mysterious force and could turn into primitive, wild animals. The compatriots in the primeval relics were mostly non-combatants, experts, scholars, and researchers. Although they cracked a lot of combat techniques of the ancient civilization and repaired some war machines, they never thought that they could deal with their compatriots.

The tomb watcher said, “Therefore, the attackers were unstoppable, while the defenders were on the verge of destruction. If the space gaps leading to the City of Silver were not so narrow that the troops of the attackers could not be deployed, I’m afraid that the City of Silver would’ve fallen a long time ago. Even with the hinderance of the space gaps, the City of Silver wouldn’t have lasted more than half a month.

“In order to unlock the mysteries of the primeval relics, the researchers had sealed 90% of their emotions. They were not scared of death at all. But they hadn’t found the answer yet, and they vaguely sensed the dangers buried in the primeval relics. If their compatriots outside acted recklessly and infuriated a certain force in the primeval relics, it was very likely that the Pangu civilization would follow in the footsteps of their predecessors and vanish into nothingness.

“Therefore, the leader of the researchers decided to activate the ‘ultimate weapon’.

“It was a piece of magical equipment that had been excavated from the primeval relics and dated back to at least billions of years ago. It was connected to the core of the primeval relics. The experts of the Pangu Civilization studied it for a hundred years and finally figured out its usage. It seemed to be able to alter the curvature of time in a small area.

“I don’t know what the curvature of time is, either. But according to my friend, time in the primeval relics will be frozen temporarily. Like an invisible amber, time will seal everybodythe ruthless researchers, the crazy attackers, the relics and corpses of hundreds of ancient civilizations, the captives of the Flood Tide Legion, and the brand-new creatures that are duplicated from the genes of the captives—in a way that is more advanced, safe, and prolonged than common hibernation.

“Although that was the theory, it was still a piece of magical equipment created by the black wall after all. The leader of the researchers was not confident that it would still be as effective when it fell into the hands of the Pangu Civilization, instead of turning everyone into ashes instantly or sealing them in the river of time forever. They would not turn into amber but into an impenetrable pebble that would sink into the mud at the bottom of the river.

“Therefore, it is very likely that this is our farewell.

“According to my friend, she once heard her father and colleagues talking about the problem of ‘time curvature’. According to her speculation, I was inextricably linked to the maker of the black wall in the first place, and I had a natural tolerance for ‘time freeze’. Besides, I was about to enter hibernation, and my physiological functions were gradually declining and converging. Therefore, I was less affected by the ‘time freeze’. If the ‘time curvature radius’ gradually returned to normal one day and the frozen river of time started flowing again, I would very likely be the first to wake up.

“She injected some special nutrition into my hibernation drug to make sure that I could continue to absorb the nutrition and grow and blossom during my hibernation. Then, she tapped my crystal tower with her tail, hoping that I would go to the outside world and see her real hometown, the civilization that she and her father and all the researchers tried to save even at the cost of their own lives.

“This was her only wish and also the last time I saw my… friend.

“She retracted her tail and left without looking back. Not long after, I sensed that invisible ripples were spreading out in the space, no, time. The ripples turned into waves, and the waves turned into swirls. At first, the swirls were spinning at a high speed, so fast that it was dazzling and dazzling. But as the countless swirls collided and melted, they gradually slowed down from a hundred small swirls to a giant one, until they came to a halt in the end. “Time froze in the primeval relics, and I fell into a long hibernation. The muddleheaded, unpredictable darkness.

In front of Li Yao’s eyes, the time in the picture gradually blurred, as if the entire primordial relic had been injected with a layer of thick and soft transparent jelly. The golden meatball stopped fluctuating and even shrank slightly, like a bright golden cell embedded in amber.

“I don’t know how long I slept in the endless darkness. The gravekeeper continued, “I only know that I’m not really ‘dead’. Occasionally, there will be waves in my almost frozen brain, in which broken, colorful pieces are blinking. There are pictures of the human civilization billions of years ago stomping on the stars and shaking the universe. There are also the times when I exchanged information streams with my friends when we were alone. I tried my best to remember all of them, as if they constituted the real ‘me’.

“However, the erosion of time is something that no force can resist after all. After being frozen and solidified for hundreds of thousands of years, my embryo didn’t receive enough nutrition and gradually withered. The techniques that were once easy for me disappeared one by one. My sharp senses and thinking became sluggish and wrong. I even lost my clear memories and forgot who I was. I forgot the origins of the primeval laboratories and the primordial relics. I even forgot the name ‘Black Wall Maker’.

“If I had to use a word to describe it precisely, it would be like… the original me was in the state of ‘50% Awakening’, and after hundreds of thousands of years of consumption and erosion, only ‘10% Awakening’ was left. I became a baby again, a real baby.

“If this state continues for another hundreds of thousands of years, perhaps I will really forget everything. Even the flashback of the last scene will be obliterated.

“Thankfully, at this moment, the frozen time inside the primeval relics was gradually thawed.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

“The so-called ‘time’ is something that is hundreds of times more complicated than ‘space’. The deities and devils of the multiverse who boast unparalleled techniques might be able to stop a ripple in the river of time temporarily and reduce the speed of the ripple. However, such an approach will inevitably consume a lot of energy and destroy the fundamental law of stability of time and space. Besides, it will not last long. “Besides, the researchers of the Pangu Civilization have only scratched the surface of how to change the curvature of time. They have tried their best to stall the war that is destined to destroy Silver City for hundreds of thousands of years.

“Just like that, in some places, the curvature of time gradually returned to normal, as if countless bubbles of time were popping up. Finally, I broke out of the cocoon and fell to the ground!

In the space full of ‘transparent jelly’ in front of Li Yao’s eyes, countless transparent bubbles suddenly popped up out of thin air. The bubbles exploded and turned into tiny swirls, like gears that had been sealed for a long time. They shrieked and revolved slowly again.

In such a weird picture, the golden ball of meat split in the middle, and a baby with bright golden skin appeared out of nowhere.

There was innocence on the baby’s face, but there was also confusion that belonged to an adult intelligent life. There was even melancholy that he did not realize.

He did not cry or laugh. He simply stretched his arms and yawned. Bright gold ripples were flashing and flowing around him.

Maybe it was because the time displayed on the picture had been accelerated by a hundred times, or maybe it was because he had awakened 10% of the ancient techniques, but he was born to grow faster than ordinary babies. Before long, the baby had grown into a child of five or six years old. He broke the crystal tower and crawled out.

He explored the world around him curiously. Every piece of equipment and magical equipment in the laboratory became his toys.

He was enjoying himself so much that he forgot that he had scanned the world around him carefully when he was still an embryo hundreds of thousands of years ago.

He strode forward with difficulty with his short, fat legs, as if he were moving forward under the weight of billions of tons of seawater. It seemed that time had not been fully unfrozen yet. Even though he had the blood of the black wall maker, he was not strong enough to resist the power of time.

Following the Supreme Emperor’s perspective, Li Yao left the laboratory for the first time and saw the entire Silver City.

As it turned out, they were standing on a ball-shaped fortress around the ‘Heaven’s Path’, overlooking the entire battlefield.

The entire Silver City was silent. It was like a chess board that had been sealed in an ice block. Countless ferocious beasts such as ‘Rampaging Armored Dragons’ and war machines that were even larger than the ‘Iron Knights of Starry Skies’ were frozen quietly, as if they were chess pieces that had been sealed for a long time. Even their broken limbs and blood were frozen in midair, wreathed in a layer of vague grey mist, making them look dark and unreal.

The bright golden child was the only bright color in this gray world.

He walked alone among the giant statues of ferocious beasts and war machines, as if he were the only living creature in the world. The scene was both weird and terrifying.

In the beginning, he had wanted to shake the frozen giants hard and wake them up so that he could ask more questions. But every time his hand touched the grey mist that wreathed the giants, it seemed to be blocked by a barrier of time and could not reach inside.

He was in a different time curve from the ‘Sleepers’. Before the radius of their curvature was synchronized, he was destined to be unreachable.

Chapter 3056: The Beginning Of Legends

“At that time, I had already forgotten everything. Everything I’m telling you right now was only gradually recalled when I was old and dying and returned to the primeval relics. But at that time, I was just an ignorant child.

The gravekeeper’s voice came over. “A child is born to be fearless. Without guidance and lessons, no weirdness or fear can stop him from taking risks. I don’t realize how bloody and terrifying this frozen battlefield is at all. I only feel that the ferocious beasts, giants, and war machines are all very interesting toys.

“I travelled in the quiet City of Silver and played in the frozen, bloody battlegrounds. I pursued the time bubbles that were popping up out of nowhere and captured the gaps in time that were gradually cracking. Finally, I found some items that were in line with my ‘time curvature’. Food, nutrition drugs, magical equipment that were small shuttles, and so on. As long as I gritted my teeth and burnt my soul, I would be able to drag them out of the twisted grey mist and into my time curvature.

Pictures were flashing in front of Li Yao’s eyes again.

The bright gold child was like him in the old days—the little scavenger born in the magical equipment graveyard who was constantly collecting the resources needed for survival between the bloody battlefield and the time gap.

The bright gold child was absorbing the nutrition drugs and strengthening drugs that were pulled out of the grey mist. He was refining the precious energy while he was dancing and waving his hands. Occasionally, he took super-compressed nutrition drugs that were not suitable for him and Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures that were hard to digest. He would also roll among mountains of corpses and seas of blood while holding his stomach. However, his natural immunity and endurance allowed him to persist time and time again, turning him into a wild child who was born and raised by nature. When he retrieved a lot of magical equipment from the grey mist-chainswords, vibration sabers, crystal processors, crystal suits, and even the debris of starships—they became his best toys again.

The bright gold young man often dismantled all the magical equipment into broken components with his telepathic thoughts and studied their structure carefully. Then, he put them together smoothly. He was already an expert in magical equipment refining at such a young age. After he discovered the navigation system of a starship, he was even more fascinated by the splendid map of the sea of stars. He often sat in the middle of the grey battlefield, triggering the splendid sea of stars from the navigation system, staring at the glittering sailing routes and the denseness of the colorful wormholes in silence, deep in thought. “I almost forgot everything,my memories when I was still in the embryo state, the strength of the human civilization billions of years ago, the destruction of almost a hundred civilizations, and the civil war of the Pangu civilization. I forgot everything. The gravekeeper said casually, “But I vaguely remember that I promised someone that I would go to the outside world in her place and see if there was a more brilliant civilization in the depths of the brilliant stars, and what she was trying her best to protect.

“The promise was like a sharp lightning that triggered certain pieces of memory buried deep inside my gene strands. In the blink of an eye, endless information flooded into my brain, allowing me to master part of the skills of starship maintenance and piloting without a teacher.

“I repaired a small shuttle and modified it into a mode that only one person could steer. Then, guided by some unknown force, I left the primeval relics and rode in the vast sea of stars of the Pangu Universe.

In front of Li Yao’s eyes, the City of Silver was collapsing and dispersing like dancing butterflies, only to be replaced by the boundless sea of stars, the brilliant stars, and the unpredictable, deadly wormholes.

But a streak of vague goldness passed through everything and brought the boy who had awakened 10% of the ancient bloodline to the center of the sea of stars.

“I overestimated my own ability. Or rather, I am not a natural product but a weirdo born in a laboratory. My body is quite deformed in every regard.

The gravekeeper spoke calmly, but Li Yao could hear a faint sorrow in his calmness. “I was stimulated by all kinds of messy experiments and awakened too much wisdom in advance that I shouldn’t have. But my body is far from being able to keep up with the intensity of the wisdom.

“For example, it is true that I repaired and modified a single-person shuttle with my natural talent, but I forgot to improve the hardness of the protective devices when I was modifying the shuttle subconsciously. The single-person shuttle was prepared for the real black-wall makers who had awakened 100% of their ancient bloodlines. The people who were as strong as gods and devils were even able to shuttle among the raging tides of the four-dimensional space freely without any protection. They controlled their every cell and every telepathic thought precisely, chasing after the clouds and striking the lightning. It was not hard to imagine that such people did not care about the ‘protection devices’ at all and could pass through the wormholes easily.

“But I can’t.

“I’ve only awakened 10% of the primeval bloodline. My body and soul are too young, and I haven’t fully recovered from the damage caused by the prolonged hibernation and the freezing of time. I can’t bear the pressure of the wormholes and the tearing of the four-dimensional storm at all.

“When I realized that I had made a terrible mistake, it was already too late. I was sucked into the turbulence of the four-dimensional space and lost my direction. After a series of soul-stirring, life-threatening compressions, tearing, spinning, crushing, and consolidation, I was tossed out of another unknown wormhole and ended up at the edge of the sea of stars.

“This is my greatest misfortune, but it is also my greatest luck.

“Unfortunately, due to the continuous heavy wounds, my abilities have been further suppressed. The golden aura around me has vanished without a trace. Most of the time, I am no different than the most ordinary human child.

“Fortunately, when the starship debris and I drifted to a chaotic asteroid mine, I met my foster parents, two very kind human beings.

“That was… the end of the ‘Great Dark Age of Thirty Thousand Years’ ten thousand years ago.

“Ah!” Li Yao exclaimed.

He knew that, from this moment on, the legend of the Supreme Emperor would truly blossom into the first dazzling ray of light on a pitch-black stage.

“It was a slave mine under the control of the demon race. At the end of the Demon Beast Dynasty, the deprivation and racial oppression were even more severe. The working conditions and living environments of the slave mines were extremely harsh. Human beings were like dry leaves that could be blown away easily.

The gravekeeper continued, “My foster parents were ordinary miners in the slave mines. However, my foster father was particularly good at steering the outdated mining ships and chasing after the rare minerals that were the most radioactive in the meteoroid storms. Therefore, he was granted special permission by the mine owner to go to places far away from the main mining area to pick up the scraps. That was where he found me. “At that time, he and my foster mother’s only child had just died due to malnutrition. My foster mother was heartbroken, and my foster father was sighing in sorrow. When he discovered that I was still breathing and my heart was beating in the debris, he was overjoyed and considered me to be a child bestowed upon him by the heavens.

“He made up his mind without any hesitation. He hid me among the humming components of the mining ship and brought me back to live as my brother who died too young.

“Although the environment in the slave mines is harsh, as long as the fixed amount of mining is completed, the owners and supervisors will not care about the life and death of a small child. Everyone will live and die on their own. Occasionally, a few miners will know my true identity, but they are all very kind-hearted people. They regard my arrival as a gift from the heavens to all the miners. They also help my foster parents and raise me together.

“Just like that, I grew up among the… dirty, rude, passionate, and kind miners and became one of them.

“Although I gradually awakened all kinds of unbelievable abilities while I was growing up, and I vaguely sensed the difference between me and the people around me, I still failed to recall who I was because of the time freeze and the wormhole storm. Or rather, I was defining myself with new experiences, family, and companions.

“One thing that you may find hard to understand ten thousand years later is that the so-called ‘slave miners’ are not just human beings but also many demons. In the depths of the dark and cruel asteroid belt, ‘race’ is simply meaningless. They are even weapons that the rulers use to drive a wedge between them and the rulers.

“It’s perfectly normal. The slave owners and rulers are so cruel to human beings that they regard the intelligent creatures as dust and ants. Do you think that they will treat demons of a lower level kindly and humanely?

“No. At the end of the Demon Beast Dynasty, the so-called Demon Empire fell apart a long time ago. All the governors and generals of the demon race declared themselves to be kings and killed each other. The losers in such a civil war are even more miserable than the slaves of human beings. After all, in the eyes of the leadership of the demon race, human beings are the most obedient creatures. They will never resist, and they pose no threat.

“The demon captives who were defeated were often deprived of all their abilities. Some of their organs were even removed and shackled before they were sent to slave mines to be tortured together with the human miners.

“There is no reproductive isolation between most human beings and demons. In such a situation where we are in the same boat, even though we hated each other’s guts in the past, it did not take long before the hatred was gone. The children of human beings and demons were born, too, who boasted even weirder and more unbelievable techniques than me.

Therefore, I was less eye-catching when I mingled with the ‘half-demons’.

Chapter 3057: Path Of Rising

In front of Li Yao’s eyes, although it was bizarre, it was a harmonious picture. Deep inside the dark and cold tunnel in the thin, man-made membrane of the asteroid belt, a child whose eyes were glittering in gold was hanging out with a bunch of half-human-half-demon children.

Just like the adult miners, they had shouldered the burden of life too early and allowed the whips and shackles to pierce deep into their flesh. Under the threat of berating, whipping, and death, they grew tenaciously like weeds and cockroaches. In the rare free time, the child whose eyes were glittering with gold gradually became the leader of all the other children. He led the children to collect the debris of the broken mining magical equipment in the depths of the mines, repair them, and modify them into all kinds of weird new magical equipment. He also led the children to deal with the supervisors and the mine owners in various ways to buy more food and rest time.

Deep inside the young man’s eyes, there was too much maturity that did not belong to his age or even his era.

The new magical equipment refined by him out of the remains of outdated magical equipment was far beyond the category of ‘mining magical equipment’. Even the crystal tanks that the supervisor rode on could be pierced through easily.

Gradually, when he and his companions turned from children into teenagers and from teenagers to teenagers, they were no longer loose sand. Instead, he had gathered them into an iron fist, a saber, an army, and a bunch of furious, lawless thugs who were willing to perish together with the stars! “When I grew up in the asteroid mines, the storm of resistance swept from the center of the sea of stars to every world under the reign of the demon race. Resistance groups and rebels appeared in our asteroid mines, too. However, at that time, the resistance groups and the rebels were still at a very low level. They were not so much well-trained warriors as civilians who were roaring because they could not bear it any longer but did not know what to do after that.

The picture that the gravekeeper presented was gradually dyed with intense redness. “Like other miners, my foster parents joined the resistance and participated in a strike on a large scale. The resistance at that time lacked clear goals and ideologies, and they never thought of overthrowing the reign of the demon race with violence. They only hoped to negotiate with the mine owner to reduce the astronomical mining quota and improve the working and living environment of the miners in order to reduce the death rate. That was all.

“My foster parents and my uncles all thought that such reasonable conditions would definitely be accepted and that there would be room for negotiation.

“But they’ve underestimated the despicableness, cruelty, and shamelessness of their rulers. Or rather, they don’t know how crazy and brutal their rulers are when they are faced with a surging tide of resistance. “Faced with the demands of the miners, the mine owner pretended to agree at first. He only said that he needed time to negotiate with the miners and pacify them temporarily. But it was just a stalling tactic. When the fully-armed army arrived at the asteroid mine, the mine owner immediately turned hostile and started a bloody slaughter.

“More than half of the miners, including my foster parents, were butchered.

“My friends and I joined the resistance, too. But in front of the well-trained professional soldiers and the fierce arsenals, our half-awakened abilities and the messy talents of my friends were absolutely useless.

“I remember that I tore apart dozens of biochemical beasts bare-handed. My brain almost spontaneously combusted because of over-exhaustion. The protective mechanism of my body was triggered automatically, and I passed out.

An unbelievably hot-blooded scene appeared in front of Li Yao’s eyes. The young man with bright gold eyes had a layer of short hair growing on his lips. The edges of his face were gradually revealed, too. The bones of his body were starting to show, too.

In the meantime, the bulging muscles all over his body when his blood was boiling in fury were even more unbelievable than the reactive plate armor of crystal suits. He seemed to be wearing armor made of flesh and blood outside of his normal body.

His hair, which had been standing up like iron needles, was dancing crazily. He was jumping up and down on the surface of the planet in fury, searching for the traces of the biochemical beasts and the experts of the demon race everywhere. Even though the barrage was raining upon him, he did not care at all. He dashed through the gaps of the bullets and drew close to the enemy.

Li Yao had seen him blow up the most solid bone armor of a biochemical beast more than ten meters tall barehanded.

Then, they saw him carrying a demon beast that weighed at least dozens of tons and rushing forward like a natural hammer, smashing all the demons who failed to dodge into meat pies.

It was not until all the demons on the battlefield considered him to be a monster and bombarded him with all their firepower that he finally staggered and fell to the ground despite the heavy fire. His blood, which was bright gold, had soaked the entire planet.

“I’m very lucky.

“If it were ten thousand years in the middle of the ‘Great Dark Age of Thirty Thousand Years’, when the reign of the demon race was at its peak and the social structure was relatively stable, monsters such as myself would’ve been sent to the most advanced biochemical laboratories to be dissected and studied.

“But by the end of the Great Dark Age, the reign of the demon race had already collapsed. The demon emperors and the demon gods were minding their own business and planning for their own benefits.

“Our mine owner lost almost half of his miners in the slaughter to suppress the insurrection. The mining base was a mess, too. There was no telling when production would resume. The daily economic losses were astronomical. He was in so much pain that he wanted to die.

“The amazing abilities that I showed in the battle made his eyes light up. He thought that he had everything he needed. Not only did he not kill me, he even helped me treat and replenish my nutrition. Of course, he did not forget to plant dozens of barriers that he thought were ‘absolutely safe’ inside my body. “After a while, my surviving companions and I were sold to an arena at an unbelievably high price. “It was a place named ‘Death Valley’, perhaps the most bloody, dark, and stinky place in the Great Dark Age of thirty thousand years. “I fought with all kinds of nightmare beasts in the ‘Valley of Death’ and participated in cruel and insane competitions. It was the strongest, most dangerous, and most likely to swallow its master.

Chapter 3067: Gold Message

Supreme Emperor!

A well-known name, a dominating name, a name that could shock all the heroes in the sea of stars, including the federation, the imperium, and the federation, Ding Lingdang, Long Yangjun, Boss Bai, Li Linghai, Li Jialing, and the boxing champion!

The golden sun of the Supreme Emperor could be said to be the most famous ultimate war weapon in the sea of stars.

The brilliant final battle between him and his own Blood God was the most magnificent and influential competition in the history of human civilization.

Although ten thousand years was enough to obliterate countless traces of the ‘golden sun’ and the ‘apocalyptic apocalypse’, the traces of obliteration had turned into even more exaggerated and glamorous legends, which made countless people remember the formidability of the ‘golden sun’ from countless perspectives. Naturally, its appearance and features were well-acknowledged and became the ultimate dream of all the Exos.

The brilliance, brilliance, toughness, and dominance of the giant soldier in front of him were beyond the imagination of the legendary ‘Gold Sun’.

Besides the Supreme Emperor himself, who else could drive such an ultimate war machine?

Watching the golden streaks of brightness splitting and interweaving in the sky again and slaying the second wave of ferocious beasts that were trying to soar into the sky within three seconds, the sharp aura of the saber, the lightning-like speed, and the precise trajectories, all made the hearts of many experts shake. They were intoxicated, and their hearts were beating fast. Even the ‘Fist King’, a mechanical expert who clearly did not have any breathing organs, felt suffocated.

swers

Beside the ‘heavenly tower’.

When the beast was roaring miserably, it unsheathed the Anti-Ship Saber unhurriedly and slashed at the internal organs of the beast along its throat. The golden flames of war surged out and burnt all the internal organs of the beast.

The griffin died before it had the chance to struggle.

The ‘sun catastrophe’ took the opportunity to crawl into the stomach of the ferocious beast and stab into the nearest black spot with the dead body of the beast.

The feeling of passing through the black spots was completely different from the feeling when one was using ordinary teleportation arrays or even space jumps.

It was like the ‘sun catastrophe’ was sealed in a piece of sticky candy that was about to be solidified. One had to exert all their strength to slowly move forward.

Thankfully, the ‘sticky candy’ was not very thick. The suffocating feeling only lasted three seconds before Li Yao felt that his entire body was relaxed. The resistance was gone, and the world around him was magnified by ten thousand times.

The dead body of the griffin beast was torn apart and burnt to ashes by the golden flames of the ‘sun catastrophe’, turning into bloody mist and dispersing.

Riding the ‘sun catastrophe’, Li Yao successfully broke into the ‘heavenly tower’.

However, what he saw inside the ‘heavenly tower’ was truly shocking and unbelievable.

Li Yao had thought that, since it was called ‘tower’, the space inside must be very limited. At best, it would be a complicated 3D maze that was designed to create visual obstacles.

eve

Judging from the outside of the ‘heavenly tower’, however large it was, it could not be more than twenty kilometers in diameter. Besides, the higher it was, the smaller its diameter should be.

Li Yao saw a brand-new sky inside the ‘heavenly tower’.

It seemed that he would be able to reach the upper level by breaking through the thunder-filled sky. It was only natural.

Many ferocious beasts flapped their enormous wings and flew toward the place where the lightning was densest. Then, they were pierced through by the sharp blades condensed by the lightning and fell into the endless darkness deep inside the valley.

Chapter 3069: Super Heavy Sea

“The stairs should not be on the top. This is a world fragment, an independent space. The so-called ‘up, down, left, right’ does not mean much. The stairs leading to the higher floors of the ‘Tower Tower’ are likely to be teleportation arrays.

Li Yao thought to himself silently, The defenders did not deploy too many war machines in the sky. The vast majority of the defenders are scattered around the valley. It can be seen that they don’t place too much importance on the sky and simply let the ferocious beasts seek their own death. I don’t need to repeat the same mistake.

He continued scanning the fluctuating canyon, only to discover that bolts of lightning were falling one after another, turning into erratic balls of lightning that devoured the lives of the ferocious beasts to their heart’s content. Even the war machine on the defending side would be paralyzed on the spot when it came into contact with the balls of lightning. It could not have been more dangerous.

However, amidst the countless explosions of lightning, Li Yao noticed an extremely unusual scene.

Every ten to twenty seconds, more than a hundred lightning bolts would hit the same place and condense into an enormous ball of lightning more than a hundred meters in diameter.

The ball of lightning, on the other hand, did not float like a regular lightning but stayed where it was, emitting glamorous colors. Ripples were flowing on its surface, making it look like a swirl or… a gate.

“There it is!”

Li Yao’s heart suddenly skipped a beat.

Of course, he was not making a wild guess based on his intuition, but the result of a full-scale scan with his keen senses. The enormous ball of lightning, under the effect of the seemingly earthshaking sound and light, rippled out spiritual energy with a strong smell of ‘space ripples’, which was very similar to the ripples when the teleportation array was just activated or when the space jump just ended.

Li Yao was certain that it was a teleportation array disguised as a super-large ball of lightning!

“This teleportation array will appear every seventeen seconds for 0.78 seconds. As long as I am within a thousand meters of it, I will be able to crawl into it in time.

“The problem is that the coordinates are always uncertain. How can I be sure where it will show up next?

Li Yao stabilized the ‘sun catastrophe’, dodged the lightning that was falling from the sky, and carved a bloody path in the tide of ferocious beasts. He did not chase after the ‘lightning teleportation array’ recklessly. Instead, he split half of his attention to scan and calculate the pattern of the teleportation array.

He did not believe that teleportation arrays were random. If so, the purpose of the test would be lost.

The Anti-Ship Swords were flying up and down. The chainswords were dancing in the wind. The remote arsenal on his left arm was on full fire. Li Yao forced the beast tide back.

After ten minutes of observation, he also discovered that, before the teleportation array appeared, the corresponding purple clouds in the sky would suddenly expand and flash. Then, a bolt of lightning would be shot out, as if they were charging or shooting.

Then, as long as the position of the purple cloud and its relative relationship were determined, together with a series of complicated calculations, the coordinates of the next teleportation array would be locked.

Strands of gold threads appeared on Li Yao’s temples again, as if his cerebral nerves and cerebral vessels had all appeared on his skin.

At this moment, the overly aggressive performance of the ‘sun catastrophe’ had already attracted the attention of many ferocious beasts. A lot of giant war machines that belonged to the defenders, such as the ‘Iron Knights of Starry Skies’, turned their barrels at Li Yao.

“I’ve figured it out!”

Li Yao did not have time to shout in excitement. He took a deep breath and pushed the muscles of his legs, the power unit of the ‘sun catastrophe’, and the support structure of his legs to the maximum.

In the first step, dazzling flames burst out of the shield on the shoulder, the back, the skirt, and the feet of the soldier. The giant rock that the soldier was standing on was blown into powder silently. The soldier accelerated at an unparalleled speed and turned into a streak of brightness, leaving dozens of blurred shadows behind.

At this time, many ferocious beasts and war machines had already sensed Li Yao’s intentions.

At this point, there was nothing that could stop him!

The front, back, left, and right were the easiest places for the enemies to focus their attention.
