This place was actually—the depths of the ocean!

Never in Li Yao’s wildest dreams did he expect that the space inside the ‘Tower Tower’ would be so weird. The lower level was a valley of thunder, and the upper level was actually the depths of the ocean.

Besides, it was not an ordinary ocean that was suppressing him. The seawater here was apparently made of a lot of heavy metals, which was similar to the mercury ocean on Boneyard. Not only was the pressure terrifying, he could not even see his own fingers when he stretched out his telepathic thoughts. He could only perceive the environment within a thousand square meters. Beyond that was the mysterious ocean that was enshrouded in the fog of war.

Pressure was the most terrifying enemy in the depths of the ocean. In the ocean forty to fifty thousand meters below the surface, even the most solid giant soldier might be crushed into a thin layer of iron when it was heavily wounded and its spiritual shield collapsed.

Although the deepest trench on standard habitable planets such as Empyrean Terminus and Heaven’s Origin was usually no more than forty to fifty thousand meters, there was no telling what kind of terrifying planet the world fragment was located on. If it was a planet more than 50% the diameter of the Heaven’s Origin Sector, it would not be strange at all if the trench was more than a hundred thousand meters deep.

While Li Yao was amazed by the horror of the undersea world fragment, a black shadow suddenly passed by him silently, trying to tear his wings apart at an amazing speed.

Although Li Yao had spun the ‘sun catastrophe’ by instinct and slashed the black shadow with the edge of his saber, he was still soaked in cold sweat.

Vicious beasts!

Judging from the solid shell and the delicate stripes, it was a high-level deadly beast with a high degree of mutation. It was one of the ‘high-level soldiers’ in the beast tide.

He never thought that a ferocious beast would actually barge into this place. Li Yao could not help but tense up.

With the crazy and chaotic brains of the ferocious beasts, it was impossible for them to be intelligent enough to see through the mysteries of the ‘Thunder Valley’.

But if somebody was manipulating them from behind the scenes, say, the two leaders of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan who were apparently highly intelligent, that might not be the case.

However, there was no telling where the mastermind behind the Vicious Beast Tide and the commander of the defense team had gone. Were they in the high-pressure ocean or in the higher levels of the ‘Tower Tower’? The field of vision and perception of this place were extremely poor. Perhaps the unknown sea was full of ferocious beasts and war machines, and in such a harsh environment where fierce battles were taking place, the slightest injury could be fatal. Li Yao did not want to get involved with either side.

The seawater was as heavy as cement. He had to summon his spiritual shield to resist the pressure every second. Even a super Giant God Soldier such as ‘Solar Catastrophe’ could not stay here for a long time. Li Yao keenly sensed the subtle changes in the seawater, which seemed to be a surging undercurrent. Holding his breath, he restrained the spiritual flames of the Giant God Soldier and swam for more than ten minutes, only to discover a giant swirl in front of him.

It was like a tornado that was hanging upside down in the seawater. The undercurrent was its raging tentacles that extended to dozens of kilometers away. The seawater and the materials stored in the ocean were all transported to the surface of the ocean.

Li Yao sensed extremely furious spiritual waves inside the swirl. Not just the ripples caused by the collision of undercurrents, there were also sharp aggressive waves, which made the place look like a chaotic battlefield.

It seemed that many ferocious beasts and defenders were lurking in the swirl and attacking each other.

It was like the lake hidden in the forest was destined to be the place of death for the top predators such as wolves, tigers, and leopards!

Chapter 3070: Freezing Hell!

It seemed to be the only exit.

But it was also a fatal trap that contained endless killing intent.

Li Yao pondered for a moment, but he had no other options. He could only march forward even though he knew that tigers were lurking.

The golden flames exploded at the bottom of the dark, endless ocean. The ‘sun catastrophe’ crawled into the swirl in a streak of brightness. While waving the four stabilization wings agilely and figuring out the direction and intensity of every turbulence inside the swirl, it sprinted upward with the help of the swirl toward the brighter and brighter surface of the ocean.

Inside the swirl, just like the thunder valley a moment ago, many ferocious beasts and war machines were scattered. However, the surging brutality and the combat ability of the war machines were much higher.

It was obvious that those who were qualified to come to this place after the screening of the Thunder Valley were the elites of both attack and defense.

Even the elites of the primeval era were not enough to stop Li Yao, who had inherited the legacy of the Supreme Emperor and was riding the ‘sun catastrophe’. He was jumping and moving in the swirl unstoppably, killing countless ferocious beasts and destroying countless war machines. After swimming at least ten thousand meters, even the apocalyptic body that had been tempered into steel felt that its lungs were aching, and its chest was about to explode.

But the pressure on his body had been greatly reduced. The seawater above his head gradually turned from deep blue to light blue, and the swirl was spinning faster and faster. He was almost thrown out of the swirl.

“It’s out!”

Li Yao roared and jumped out of the ocean.

But what he saw dumbfounded him even more.

He had thought that he would find the exit, or at least the traces of the exit, after he jumped out of the ocean.

In the worst-case scenario, seeing a vast, boundless ocean would not be as depressing as when he was in the deep ocean.

But there was nothing but ocean in front of his eyes. There was no concept of horizon or sky at all.

Within the range of his vision, the ocean extended into the distance before it slightly curved upwards like an arc-shaped giant wave that affected the entire ocean. It rose all the way to what should have been the sky before it swept back from another direction and blocked all directions.

To put it more frankly, it was like an enormous water ball the size of a planet. In the middle of the water ball was a giant bubble.

Li Yao had merely crawled into the bubble at the center of the water ball from the inside. If he continued flying upward, he would only fall into the seawater on the other side after he passed through the center. It was meaningless. “Are you kidding me?” Li Yao felt dizzy. Was there no exit at all?

It was impossible. The exit must be hidden somewhere, somewhere that defied common sense and intuition…

Li Yao held his breath and observed the ball-shaped ocean.

He discovered that, other than the giant swirl below his feet, there were more than ten swirls of the same size above his head and on the surface of the ocean on his left and right. Countless ferocious beasts had been swept away by the swirls and flown to the center of the ‘big water ball’.

On the contrary, the war machines that belonged to the defenders were relatively rare, which also confirmed Li Yao’s judgement-the defenders had already deciphered the secrets of this world fragment and knew the correct exit.

Let’s say that this world fragment is really an enormous water ball with a tiny bubble floating in the middle, and I am an insignificant microorganism living inside the water ball. How can I leave the water ball?

Li Yao thought hard for a while. Then his eyes suddenly shone. “Right. If I march toward the center of the water ball along the swirl, there is no way that I can leave the water ball. I can only reach another ocean and another swirl.”

“Go down! I should go down!”

“Which ocean is not important, which swirl is not important, and which direction is important. We should go against the swirl and reach the end of the swirl in the deepest part of the ocean!”

Li Yao lowered his head and glanced at Swirl, who looked like a bloody mouth.

It was easy to say, but it was not easy to make up his mind to explore the end of the swirl.

Deep inside the swirl, in the dark ocean tens of thousands of meters deep, there might be an even deeper undersea canyon that could crush the Giant God Soldiers, crystal suits, and even the drivers into mud easily.

Although the road ahead was full of unknown risks, Li Yao still decided to believe in his own judgement.

Before countless ferocious beasts lunged at him while roaring, Li Yao jumped into the swirl and returned the way he came.

Against the direction of the swirl, it was ten times more difficult to face the attacks of countless ferocious beasts.

Despite the sturdiness and toughness of the ‘sun catastrophe’, it was still seriously wounded by the ferocious beasts and the war machines when it returned to the bottom of the ocean twenty to thirty thousand meters below the surface. Its plate armor was twisted and riddled with wounds, and it was not able to escape the fate of being reimbursed in less than half a month if any crystal suit or soldier fell into Li Yao’s hands.

At such a depth, it was impossible to see one’s fingers in front of one’s face. The visibility and scanning range of telepathic thoughts had been reduced to the minimum. Neither Li Yao, the ferocious beasts, nor the war machines on the defense side could find each other.

In this place, the greatest enemy had become the seawater, the boundless pressure, or rather, his own soul.

The surroundings were as quiet as a deserted graveyard. Li Yao could only hear his own heartbeat and the creaking sounds of the giant soldier gods.

The iron gods and devils that used to be indestructible were now like loose bamboos. It was quite scary.

The deeper he dived, the higher the pressure became. The spiritual shield, defense structure, metal hardness, sealing degree, and other parameters of the ‘sun catastrophe’ were all on the verge of collapse. They were like candles in the wind that could be extinguished instantly.

At this point, even though Li Yao’s mind was as firm as iron, he was still unable to control the bean-sized cold sweat on his forehead.

“Was my judgement correct?

“Is the exit really not on the surface of the ocean but under the sea?

Is there an end to the ocean? Did I miss the exit and fall into an undersea canyon leading to the deepest level of hell?

“Is it possible that the ‘sun catastrophe’ suffered too much damage in the battle just now? Even though the exit is right below me, the hardness of the metal cannot hold on any longer. Will I be crushed into a meat pie near the exit?”

Such thoughts leaked out of Li Yao’s every pore like black, sticky shadows and tied him up. They murmured, screamed, and screamed in his ears.

Thankfully, a second personality that specialized in absorbing negative emotions such as the mental devil split out from Li Yao’s soul and swallowed all the distracting thoughts that bothered his soul and corrupted his mind.

“Don’t think about such nonsense. The mental devil shrieked, “Do you still want to go back the way you came?”

Li Yao was suddenly woken up from his dream.

Despite the screams of the giant soldier gods, Li Yao gritted his teeth and kept diving.

He felt that he was climbing a cliff in the deepest level of hell. One moment of carelessness, and he would fall into an abyss beyond redemption.

However, no matter how furious the devil fire in the abyss was, it could not stop his determination to march forward.

BOOM!

Dull noises suddenly echoed below the feet of the ‘sun catastrophe’. Li Yao felt that his heart was in his throat. He was scared that the Giant God Soldier could not bear the pressure any longer and was about to collapse. But then he sensed that he had reached the bottom of the ocean safely.

Not far away from him, right below the swirl, was a glittering super teleportation array.

This is the place!

Li Yao was overjoyed. He stood on it without any hesitation and was teleported to the third world fragment.

“What the hell is going on here?” Li Yao was already mentally prepared. He knew that the tests in the ‘heavenly towers’ would be more and more difficult.

However, although it was a cave, there was no entrance except for the teleportation array below his feet.

Of course, he could not return to the ‘weight ocean’ through the teleportation array. He could only release his telepathic thoughts and explore the smooth walls of the cave.

Soon, Li Yao found a clue.

The cave was surrounded by solid walls on three sides, except for an ice wall that had just been consolidated.

It seemed that this was the exit.

Taking advantage of the lack of interference, Li Yao took out a Cosmos Ring and activated a lot of spare components and ammunition. Then, he performed an emergency repair on the ‘sun catastrophe’ with his telekinesis ability.

It was better to be safe than sorry. Even if the performance of the Giant God Soldier was improved by 1%, it could still save his life at the critical moment.

After everything was done, he took out the electric axe and melted and cut the ice wall leading to the outside world carefully.

Hiu!

Just as he cut a hole in the ice wall, a cold wind mixed with astral gas blew in. Li Yao had never experienced such a weird wind before. Despite the four layers of protection from the Giant God Soldier, Little Black, the crystal suit, and the mustard suit, Li Yao still felt that the wind was condensing into scalpels on his bones and scraping them!

A thick ice shell immediately appeared on the surface of the ‘sun catastrophe’. Even the bright gold flames of war were half frozen.

The temperature inside the cave dropped below zero. The performance of the Giant God Soldier was plummeting, too. This is an extremely cold world. Damn!

Li Yao wanted to spit, but his throat was frozen, too.

Compared to the extremely high temperature, the extremely low temperature was more harmful for magical equipment and Cultivators. No magical equipment could be used normally in a harsh environment close to absolute zero. Naturally, no Cultivators—be they Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators, Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, or Soul Splitting Cultivators —were willing to fight in such an icy hell.

Chapter 3071: Great Avalanche

“The metal fatigue of the Giant God Soldier is soaring, as if it is being attacked by the super arsenals all the time. The rune arrays on the surface and inside are all blocked by frost. Even the fuel is freezing. How many degrees is this

place?”

Before he tore apart the ice wall, he had planned to release a few telepathic thoughts to investigate.

However, the moment his telepathic thoughts crawled out of the gap, they were tangled by the wind that was close to absolute zero. Like invisible blades, they crawled into his brain through his telepathic thoughts and scratched the inside of his skull hard, giving him a splitting headache. His soul was in a mess, and he was barely able to control the giant soldier. It was indeed a deadly frigid hell!

Even so, it was impossible for him to curl up in this place for the rest of his life. What’s more, along the gap where he had just melted, spider-net cracks were spreading and spreading on the ice wall. Even if he didn’t take the initiative to go out, the wind could still shatter the ice wall.

Thankfully, he had repaired the Giant God Soldier in a hurry. Otherwise, the first round of wind would have frozen the core units of the ‘sun catastrophe’.

Even Lu Qingchen and the mental devil, the two remaining souls, were shivering in the cold. Their soul waves were almost frozen, and their communication with Li Yao was intermittent

No. The ground and the sky had been completely frozen. Li Yao looked into the distance, only to discover that the sky was covered in snow. He could not tell where the horizon was exactly.

The moment the Giant God Soldier took a step forward, he was already buried in snow more than ten meters deep. It was quite fortunate. It was quite possible that bottomless valleys and cliffs were below the snow. If he stepped on nothing, the Giant God Soldier would be buried deep below the ground by billions of tons of snow and ice. Even if he managed to escape, his performance, structural strength, and fuel reserves would be greatly undermined.

Then, what about flying into the sky? Li Yao tried to control the ‘sun catastrophe’ to fly one to two hundred meters away, only to be suppressed by the wind that was getting more and more intense. Faced with the most furious power of nature, even the Giant God Soldier that the Supreme Emperor once commanded could not crash into anything. Li Yao felt that he was nothing more than a fly that had been slapped down by invisible flies.

“Pu!”

The ‘catastrophe of the sun’ fell into the snow again, and the world was spinning.

However, Li Yao took the opportunity to scan the place.

Maybe because the volcanic eruption had blown a lot of dust into the sky and formed a thick gray shell that blocked all the energy radiation, the temperature of the place was frighteningly low.

The snow mountain was larger and more magnificent than any mountain Li Yao had ever seen. ‘Towering into the clouds’ was not enough to describe the magnificence of the mountain at all. It was as if the mountain was not piercing into the clouds at all, but all the clouds in the sky were shivering and kneeling before it.

It seemed that the snow mountain was still active. A lot of heavy metal and rare elements were spurting out of the crater of the volcano, demonstrating its irresistible strength in the clouds.

Li Yao’s eyes were sharp. When he looked down from the top of the mountain, he saw countless black spots wriggling on the snow and climbing to the top of the mountain. They were the war machines of the attackers and the defenders.

It never occurred to him that the guy who attacked the City of Silver would be so smart and capable as to crack the mysteries of ‘Thunder Valley’ and ‘Gravity Sea’ and march all the way here.

Could it be that the secret through this level was hidden in the crater of the Snowy Peak?

This time, Li Yao did not act recklessly.

The snow was heavy, and there was not much time. If he did not climb to the top of the mountain, where could he find the clues? More importantly, if the snow mountain was really a volcano, the temperature near the top of the mountain must be much higher than here, and the giant soldier gods could persist longer.

Thinking of this, Li Yao took a deep breath and joined the team climbing the snow mountain.

Gloomy wind was blowing. Ice crystals were swirling. On the magnificent snow mountain, countless ferocious beasts and war machines almost a hundred meters tall were wriggling, struggling, and climbing up like insignificant black spots. What an unbelievable and magnificent scene it was!

Originally, Li Yao only wanted to bury his head in climbing. He didn’t want to compete with the enemy in this kind of environment, even though he had a very favorable shooting position and could calmly poke the ass of the ferocious beasts or war machines.

Other than the lack of ammunition and the deterioration of the performance of the Giant God Soldiers, there was one more reason-an avalanche!

Yes. In the current environment, an avalanche was a hundred times more deadly than thunder, storms, and tsunamis.

The snow on the snow mountain had accumulated for countless years. The ice crystals in many places had been frozen and compressed to the point that they were more solid than rocks. If the battle triggered an explosion and the explosion triggered an avalanche, billions of tons of ice crystals and rocks would roll down at the same time. The consequences would be dire. However, although Li Yao knew the consequences of triggering an avalanche, the ferocious beasts and war machines did not.

They were still crashing into each other above Li Yao’s head unscrupulously. They were yelling and yelling, unleashing dazzling electric arcs and fireballs.

Naturally, when a ferocious beast almost a hundred meters tall or a war machine weighing almost ten thousand tons collapsed, a series of small-scale avalanches would be triggered, too. Sometimes, the snow, ice, and rocks would roll down from above Li Yao’s head like a flood and brush past him. He was greatly shocked and soaked in cold sweat.

“Can’t you guys stop for a moment and wait until you’ve climbed to the top of the mountain before fighting? Do you have to create an earthshaking avalanche to be happy?”

Li Yao gnashed his teeth and screamed in his heart.

His words were like a spell that activated the mysterious force hidden deep inside the snow mountain.

The entire snow mountain suddenly started shaking.

It was not the scourge of the explosion on the surface of the snow mountain, but the uncontrollable quakes inside the mountain that seemed to be a chain reaction.

On the top of the snow mountain, the colorful clouds had been burnt into a raging crimson color at some point. Intense lead ash was spreading out like a tide and soon swallowed half of the sky, casting a suffocating shadow over the entire snow mountain.

It was the smoke from the depths of the volcano!

“Are you serious?”

Li Yao’s eyes bulged out as he mumbled to himself, “I can’t be so unlucky as to encounter a volcanic eruption that I haven’t seen in tens of thousands of years. Is this a coincidence, or

Once they exploded, it would immediately trigger a chain reaction that would infuriate the entire snow mountain and trigger a destructive avalanche that would bury all the enemies who dared to trespass!

Billions of tons of magma spurted out of the volcano and burnt the sky into a dazzling ocean of red lotuses. Then, it broke into pieces and smashed the earth like a storm of meteorites.

Chapter 3072: Working Together

The snow mountain immediately turned into a hell of red and white.

Many ferocious beasts and war machines did not expect that a volcano that had been dormant for thousands of years would suddenly erupt and be hit by the meteor shower.

Even the solid shells of the ‘Rampaging Armored Dragons’ and the defensive force fields of the ‘Star Knights’ were not enough to resist the scorching magma that pierced through their bodies like a stream of particles.

One giant object after another was riddled with holes and fell from the middle of the mountain, turning into miserable-looking snowballs that were rolling larger and larger, attracting even more unlucky beasts and war machines.

When the magma splashed on the snow slope and the icy rocks, the ice that had been frozen for thousands of years was melted and turned into an overwhelming flood. Hundreds of waterfalls seemed to have appeared on the snow mountain all of a sudden. After a moment of crashing, the waterfalls all congregated into white mud. It was an unprecedented avalanche. A magnificent white wall appeared in front of Li Yao.

Run, run, panic-stricken, and desperate. Countless ferocious beasts and war machines, including Li Yao, were engaged in a life-and-death race against the avalanche.

Under normal circumstances, with the super high speed that the ‘sun catastrophe’ could reach at least five times the speed of sound, there was no need to worry that the avalanche would catch up to them. In the worst-case scenario, they could even fly into the sky to avoid the avalanche.

However, the extremely low temperature of this frigid hell, as well as the ever-present frigid winds, nearly froze every magical equipment unit and spiritual energy transmission tube of the Giant God Soldier. The simplest movements became extremely complicated. Li Yao did not even dare to turn off the nozzle of any auxiliary power unit, because once it was turned off, the nozzle might be frozen in just three seconds.

Even the super Giant God Soldiers such as the Solar Disaster’ were no better than the ferocious beasts that had been rolling and sliding on the ground.

While Li Yao gritted his teeth and triggered the flames of war to ensure that every unit of the giant soldier was functioning normally, while he tried his best to avoid the magma falling from the sky and planned his escape route crazily, the mental devil suddenly shouted.

Li Yao was dazed for a moment. He split part of his telepathic thoughts and struggled in the bone-piercing wind to the direction that the mental devil was pointing at.

He saw a member of the Nuwa Clan.

On the snow mountain, many guardians of the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and other primeval species were intercepting the invading beasts in order to buy more time for the leadership to detonate the crystal bombs deep inside the mountain.

When the avalanche took place, the guardians were not spared, either. They fell together with the ferocious beasts and the war machines, struggling between the snow, the magma, the survival, and the death.

Chapter 3073: Observer

Li Yao had no time to check the specifications of the giant soldier. He scanned the girl of the Nuwa Clan who had fallen not far away first.

She fell to the ground and struggled for a long time before she managed to stand up again. Li Yao noticed that colorful glimmers were spreading out of her body like rainbows, emitting warmth and vitality.

It turned out to be a battle suit that was as thin as a cicada’s wing and almost had a second layer of skin.

It seemed that the combat suit had both the ability to absorb blast, store energy, and buffer at critical moments as well as the ability to keep the wearer warm. Otherwise, with the special physiological structure of the Nuwa Clan, which was a combination of a cold-blooded animal and a warm-blooded animal, it would’ve been frozen into a popsicle a long time ago in such an environment.

The girl of the Nuwa Clan was bold and calm beyond her peers. Li Yao took off a few Cosmos Rings very slowly and pushed them to the girl of the Nuwa Clan.

Rustling noises were echoing on the tip of the girl’s tail.

She extended a finger, gesturing for Li Yao to do the same.

Pondering for a moment, Li Yao did not refuse. He extended his left index finger toward the tail.

The tip of the girl’s tail touched Li Yao’s index finger softly. Li Yao immediately felt that his fingertip was numb, as if a drop of blood had been collected by her, or a drop of blood had been melted into his body.

Li Yao immediately felt that he knew the girl’s body and mind like the back of his hand. He knew that her internal organs had shifted and that she had suffered a moderate concussion. Besides, she had kept her sanity. She was merely curious about Li Yao and had no desire to attack him maliciously or recklessly.

In the same way, Li Yao believed that his physiological and psychological parameters were stored in the drop of blood and collected, analyzed, and digested.

So, this is how the Nuwa Clan greet each other!

Li Yao suddenly realized what was going on. “The Nuwa Clan has always been an expert in the analysis and integration of genes. By exchanging blood, they can analyze the secretion of hormones and the physiological parameters of the body in the blink of an eye. From there, they can determine the species, the level of civilization, and even the state of mind of the enemy, whether it is good or bad, etc. For example, if I have ill intentions and plot against the girl of the Nuwa Clan, the secretion of hormones in my body will definitely be abnormal because of my anxiety. Although I can control every breath and heartbeat precisely, it is impossible for me to control the secretion of hormones to the ‘absolutely good-hearted state’. Then, the enemy will be able to see through me as long as I have one drop of blood!

It was like the tail of the Nuwa Clan was a natural polygraph!

Such a method was the simplest and most effective way to break the ‘chain of suspicion’ between human beings and make sure that they were good or evil. As an alliance of multiple species, the Pangu Civilization was indeed an indispensable technique.

Of course, it was another question whether or not the exchange of blood without protection would spread multiple diseases or even lead to a great outbreak of plagues. Maybe the destruction of the Pangu civilization was partly because of that, too.

The girl of the Nuwa Clan analyzed Li Yao’s blood carefully and was even more relieved.

“See? I didn’t lie to you, did I?”

Li Yao said, “My name is Li Yao. What’s yours?”

His words were not only transmitted by his vocal cords but also by his brainwaves and telepathic thoughts.

Time was of the essence. The amount of information that the human language could carry was too little after all, and the efficiency of communication was too low. Li Yao tried to find an efficient way of communication that both sides could understand.

The girl of the Nuwa Clan blinked and hissed deep down her throat, before she spat out nine complicated syllables.

Although there were only nine syllables, they seemed to have overlapped the nine hundred syllables of the human language. Every syllable contained endless variations, like a complicated maze of information.

Along with the syllables, the girl of the Nuwa Clan sent out waves of brainwaves to strengthen the information of the syllables.

It was the same combination of language and brainwaves, but her way of communication was apparently more advanced and reasonable than Li Yao’s. Li Yao simply repeated the sentence ‘I am Li Yao. Who are you?’ in both language and brainwaves, but her brainwaves and syllables complemented and complemented each other, as if they were multiplying the language.

Li Yao had discovered traces of the language of the Pangu Civilization in the relics of Kunlun and the underground palace of Pangu. He had studied the language of the primeval era hard, too.

Together with the fact that his brain had become as powerful as an array-type super crystal processor after he advanced into the Divinity Transformation Stage. Therefore, although it was the first time that he had heard a living member of the Nuwa Clan talk, he could tell what they were trying to say.

The girl of the Nuwa Clan was also introducing herself, but her name was different from that of human beings. There was much more information in her name.

There were only two or three names of human beings, but the name of the Nuwa Clan was like a picture, a video, and even a subtle state of mind, emotions, and ambitions that were beyond description.

Such a picture popped up in Li Yao’s head. A tall, magnificent, deep-eyed Nuwa was struggling to crawl out of the mud on the primitive planet where they lived. She looked up at the sky, her eyes attracted by the enormous, bright moon, as if she had been enlightened.

It was the ancestor of the girl of the Nuwa Clan. The picture was the surname of the girl of the Nuwa Clan. If it were to be translated into the language of human beings, it could only be called ‘the Observer of the Moon’. However, such simple and crude words diminished most of the vastness, antiquity, and depth of the picture.

In another picture, the girl and her father were looking at the grey sky of the City of Silver in the primeval relics. They wanted to look at the moon, but they could not see any celestial bodies. They had never left the place in their entire life. They had never seen the bright moon in their hometown, the splendid new moon, or the gentle moonlight. They could only lower their heads and think about their hometown and their family that they had never been to before.

Although they were sad, for the sake of their hometown and their family, they would not be able to return to their hometown and see the moon for the rest of their lives, even at the cost of their lives. They had to fulfill their destiny and continue on the dark road where the moon could not be seen.

It was the meaning of the nine syllables and the brainwave information that the girl of the Nuwa Clan had analyzed. It was also the name that her father, the leader of the ‘manufacturing project’, had given her.

Chapter 3074: 102 Fallen

In the brainwaves transmitted by the girl, Li Yao saw a middle-aged woman with a wrinkled face, deep eyes, and a calm demeanor standing in the middle of hundreds of crystal towers. He even saw the bright gold embryo that once housed the Supreme Emperor.

It appeared that the girl’s father had transformed his lifelong endeavors into a certain ‘spiritual imprint’ and embedded it into his daughter’s name. She was indeed the Supreme Emperor’s ‘old friend’.

It was certainly not a coincidence.

If the girl hadn’t sensed the scent of the Supreme Emperor from Li Yao, she wouldn’t have had to spare some of her senses to scan for the ‘sun catastrophe’ when the avalanche was coming and the destructive force was chasing after her, thereby allowing the mental devil to discover her.

Li Yao didn’t know how to explain the girl’s name in the language of human beings. No matter how he tried, he couldn’t explain the saint-like devotion, the martyr-like grief, and the fearlessness of the girl’s father.

He could only use the girl’s surname and call her ‘Moon-gazer’. Looking at Li Yao, who was deep in thought, two crescent arcs rose up in the depths of the bright eyes of the Observer. The brightness of his ‘second skin’ turned gentle and warm, bringing warmth to Li Yao.

Dozens of syllables flowed out of her lips and her teeth into Li Yao’s brain, boosted by the brainwaves.

“You are not him.”

A voice that sounded like a silver bell suddenly echoed inside Li Yao’s brain. An image of a bright gold embryo spinning slowly popped up. It was the girl who was communicating with him in such a way. “But you have an aura very similar to his. Your strength is as good as most of the elite warriors of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan.

girl was talking about was the Supreme Emperor.

But he occupies a very important place in our civilization. Almost all the modern human beings acknowledge that we are his children and that I am his direct heir. Li Yao tried to communicate with her in a somewhat clumsy way by mimicking the way that the Moon Watchers vibrated their throats and triggered their brainwaves.

Since they were all carbon-based intelligent creatures, the vocal cords of the Nuwa Clan were essentially the same as those of human beings. However, the range of the vibration frequency was much wider, allowing them to produce infrasonic and ultrasonic waves that were indistinguishable to the ears of ordinary people.

At Li Yao’s level, he was naturally able to hear the soundwaves at any frequency clearly. No matter how complicated the communication method was, it was just a matter of time before it was broken.

Naturally, at the beginning, Li Yao was stammering and had to use gestures and postures to strengthen the information.

However, the Observer was very patient and listened attentively. He even smiled and encouraged Li Yao to continue. He even sent new brainwaves to Li Yao and helped Li Yao adjust the frequency and mode of brainwave transmission while he taught Li Yao a new way of communication.

“It’s been at least five hundred thousand years since you were sealed here.

Li Yao observed her face and sensed the changes in her brainwaves and physiological parameters. He could not sense anything wrong with the girl at all. Instead, he could not hold back his curiosity and asked, “Are you not surprised at all?”

“Should I be surprised?”

The Observer smiled. “For what?”

“For… such a long time has passed, and your civilization is almost destroyed!

Chapter 3075: The End Of Childhood

It is like a transportation hub in all directions. Countless invisible tunnels leading to the four-dimensional space are interconnected in this place. It has become a maze and a riddle that hundreds of civilizations failed to resolve even after billions of years.

“Such features endowed the unpredictable features of the ancient relics. In this place, the continuity and stability of time and space have been broken. It is possible that it is a hot summer day in this place, but a hundred meters away, there is nothing but snow, or the blossoming of colorful flowers. A hundred meters away, there is a carpet of black and white fungi, which creates all kinds of rigid and rigid images. In my childhood, I used to chase butterflies through the flowers, only to find that the butterflies turned into burning flames in the blink of an eye, and the flames turned into countless stars that vanished with the wind, as if they were being reborn in the form of fireflies. When I extended my fingers to the place where the butterflies perished, my fingers turned into colorful fireworks and blossomed in the swirl of time and space.

“All in all, this is the case here. All the inherent, stable rules can be broken. Any weird thing can happen. The most disciplined and prudent experts may die a miserable death in the weirdest way, and the crazy lunatics may live longer.

“According to my father, there were only two outcomes for the first batch of researchers and adventurers to enter the ancient relics. They would either be faced with the unpredictable environment, the intimidating relics of civilization, or be scared to death by the omnipresent weird radiation and have a mental breakdown. Or, the rules, formulas, and theorems that they’d been studying for their entire lives would be crushed by the unconventional objects inside the ancient relics. They would crawl into a hole and try to capture the vague rules inside the ancient relics in order to find the objective rules. But like moths chasing the sun, they would never reach their target and end up wasting their lives in depression.

“Because of this, the researchers and adventurers who entered the primordial relics later had to seal most of their emotions and willpower in order to survive in the nightmarish relics.

It is rare for them to have a mental breakdown before they start their research. Therefore, before abnormal brainwaves are detected, it is not necessary to completely seal their emotions. While the Moongazer Serpent was talking, the City of Silver in Li Yao’s mind, on the surface of the skyscrapers that were as smooth as a mirror, was dyed with glamorous stripes, too, like blossoming flowers from a different star. The weapons, magical equipment, technology, corpses, and relics of the civilization that they left behind will be of great help to our civilization, too. They will help the Pangu civilization prolong its life as much as possible.

“Besides, the expedition fleet has captured a large batch of the Flood Tide Legion and their starships outside of the black wall. Before we analyze them in depth and figure out all the secrets of the Flood Tide Legion, it is impossible for us to bring them back to the central area of civilization, which is a highly populated area. We can only research them in a place that is isolated from the rest of the world. In such a way, the losses will be minimized if we lose control.

“Therefore, the expedition fleet was divided into two groups. Some of the best warriors and explorers stayed on the spot and established the forward bases that belonged to the Pangu Civilization, or ‘City of Silver’, according to you. They left their hometown and came to this place.

“Since the first day of their arrival, the experts and their families have been very clear that it will be difficult for them to return to their hometown for the rest of their lives.

“On one hand, the research task is too vast and tedious. There are too many treasures that are worth their entire lives. There is no way that they can finish the research.

“On the other hand, too many secrets are buried in the primeval relics, and the viruses and genetic weapons that are likely to lurk in the cells are not suitable for the public. If the secrets or the viruses spread out, it will only accelerate the destruction of the entire civilization.

“For safety reasons, after accepting dozens of batches of experts and scholars, the City of Silver has been sealed and turned into a mysterious and unique quarantine zone. The researchers and the leadership who know the inside story call it the ‘Heavenly Palace’.

Chapter 3077: Farther And Farther

An image appeared in Li Yao’s brain.

One weirdly-shaped, magnificent warship after another transported the warriors, wise men, researchers, and academic authorities of the Pangu Civilization to the entrance of the ancient relics.

Before they left the starship and entered the primeval relics, they had all signed a contract and made a solemn vow. From now on, they would be isolated from the world and explore the mysteries of the primeval civilization with all their lives and souls. They would not be able to return to their hometown until the riddle of the primeval civilization was cracked and the ‘black wall’ that bound the civilization was overturned.

In fact, the first batch of experts and scholars were very clear that, with the profoundness, mystery, and complexity of the primeval relics, they would not be able to achieve their goal even if they spent their entire lives. The best they could do was build new steps with their dead bodies so that their descendants could step on their shoulders and climb up to their destination.

Even so, they had no regrets. They abandoned everything they had in the mortal world and fled into the quarantine zone known as the ‘Heavenly Palace’.

“The so-called ‘immortals’ of the ‘paradise’ are, of course, the ‘original ancestors’, or the makers of the black wall. But as time went by, our compatriots in the outside world misunderstood that it was our way of addressing ourselves.

“Since you are also a refiner, you should have a certain understanding about materials science, spiritual energy science, aerodynamics, and other fields. Often, the proof of a formula and the discovery of a law not only require the inspiration of a genius but also mysterious luck.

work.

“However, the clansmen outside could not wait that long

There was obvious disappointment and regret on the face of the Observer.

But Li Yao did not think so.

The Observer had been living in the primeval relics since he was a child. He was a pure scholar who grew up in a laboratory. Naturally, he did not know the complexity of the world or the perils of human beings. Li Yao, on the other hand, had dealt with the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance before. His wife had been the Speaker of Parliament, too. He was well aware that it was not as simple as playing a game named ‘Civilization’ to be in charge of a civilization.

Those who are not in charge do not know the value of firewood and rice. Thousands of people would have to tighten their belts or even starve to death.

It was the turning point of the Pangu civilization’s rise and decline. In the thousands of years to come, the resources of the Pangu civilization would certainly be running low. The control of the supreme authorities would be greatly undermined.

If the Heavenly Palace could produce research results continuously, the highest authorities could still manage to maintain such a ‘deal’. But when the Heavenly Palace failed to produce any valuable research results for a hundred years, how could the highest authorities convince all the citizens to cut their belts, which had been stretched to the limit, further?

The experts and scholars of the federation and the imperium had examined the political system of the Pangu Civilization before. For most of the time, the Pangu Civilization did not have an arbitrary ruler. It was still a republic that was negotiated by various tribes. Therefore, there was not a single dictator who could make a decision to provide resources to the Heavenly Palace unconditionally, unprincipled, and unlimitedly. Any supply of resources would certainly be scrutinized and scrutinized by… institutions such as the parliament.

On the other hand, there were too many secret treasures inside the Heavenly Palace that could potentially destroy the world. No civilization with a perfect system would allow such a force to be controlled by a small group of people. Even the Observer himself had said that, after a thousand years of development, the scholars inside the Heavenly Palace and their compatriots in the outside world had drifted apart and almost evolved into two different civilizations. Therefore, when their compatriots in the outside world grew suspicious of them when the Heavenly Palace failed to produce anything of value for a hundred years, it would not be strange at all for them to march into the Heavenly Palace and conduct a thorough investigation.

Chapter 3078: The Heavenly Palace In The Storm’S Eye

Li Yao’s hesitation was clearly seen by the Observer through the transmission of brainwaves.

Why do the outsiders not understand such a simple logic?”

Chapter 3080: Breaking Through By Force

Li Yao was in a dilemma.

It was indeed as the Observer had said. The boundary between good and evil was already blurred.

He had thought that the Pangu Clan was the embodiment of pure evil and coldness, but now it seemed that the reason for the civil war was not entirely unreasonable.

Weird and terrifying things such as the primeval relics, the Heaven’s Path Tower, and the ultimate test might destroy the civilization and restart the universe if they were not careful. Other than the primeval relics, the civilization that had been destroyed could not even leave a trace. It was the most humiliating way to die.

Whoever was asked to run such a wretched test would have to consider everything carefully and carefully. A hundred examples of the victims were right in front of their eyes. The odds that the Pangu civilization could pass the test were less than 1%. As the leader of the civilization alliance, sealing the tower temporarily was indeed the most responsible choice that the Pangu Clan could make.

It seemed that Li Yao had been hesitating whether or not to establish his own exploration squad to explore the ancient relics.

If they had really come to this place and found the ‘corpses’ of the civilization, their brains would’ve been extremely calm, too, and they wouldn’t have dared to act rashly.

From this perspective, Li Yao’s choice and the Pangu Clan’s choice were actually the same.

On the other hand, Lu Qingchen was a lunatic who feared nothing and no one. Even if he knew that the failure of the test would result in the annihilation of the human civilization, he would certainly be eager to give it a try. In this matter, Lu Qingchen was on the side of the Nuwa Clan.

The question was, between the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, between Li Yao and Lu Qingchen, which one of them had made the right choice to push the wheel of history forward and save their civilization?

They didn’t know. They really didn’t know. Faced with the vast and unknown sea of stars, the only thing they knew was that they knew absolutely nothing!

Li Yao sighed and listened to the Observer.

The following story was very simple.

The people of the Heavenly Palace knew that a civil war had broken out in the outside world, and the ideology of both sides of the civil war was something that they could not agree on. The scholars who were willing to sacrifice dozens of generations of their lives to save their compatriots and their civilization were even less willing to provide destructive weapons to help the ambitious people slaughter their own people. Therefore, they chose to seal the primeval relics and completely block the Heavenly Palace from the outside world. They even thought of various ways to hide the entrances of the primeval relics so that they would be a whole different world.

For a period of time, their methods were indeed effective. No matter how heated the civil war outside was, no matter how many Sectors were burnt to the ground and how many experts perished, the storm of destruction never reached the Heavenly Palace.

It was a pity that he could not hide himself forever.

It had been a long time since the Pangu Civilization discovered the primeval relics. With the advanced space navigation technology, it was possible to map the patterns and trajectories of the entrances of the relics precisely. As the civil war grew more and more intense, the capability of the two parties plummeted. When they were about to perish together, the importance of the Heavenly Palace became more and more obvious.

At this point, it was safe to say that whoever controlled the Heavenly Palace would win the civil war and become the new master of the Pangu Civilization. They would carry out their ideology to the end.

On the other hand, if both sides of the civil war suffered heavy losses, it was very likely that the people of the Heavenly Palace would rush out of the primeval relics with their amazing technology and magical equipment, clean up the mess, and rule over the Pangu Civilization. It was absolutely intolerable for both sides of the civil war.

Therefore, the high wall at the periphery of the primeval relics, which had been sealed for almost a hundred years, was finally broken by the civil war. They pointed their last attack at the Heavenly Palace almost at the same time and were caught unprepared.

Chapter 3081: The World Of Appearance

“It concerns another important research that we are working on.

Judging from their eyes, which were as sharp as swords, it was obvious that they had not lost their mind, either. They were still eager to jump from one ‘floating ice’ to another ‘island’ and to the swirl in the center of the magma ocean. Once they jumped to the same ‘island’, a fierce battle would break out immediately.

It appeared that they were the last elites of the Heavenly Palace, the Pangu Clan, and the Nuwa Clan.

He was also the strongest competitor for the ‘ultimate test’.

Li Yao’s telepathic thoughts glanced over the experts and were soon attracted by the three figures in midair.

It was not so much three enormous figures floating less than half a meter above the ocean of magma as it was three interconnecting, entangled, and colliding death rays.

Inside the grey light beam was a Pangu Clan member who was as tall as a mountain, with a heavily deformed body on the left side and a giant lump on his shoulder that looked like two heads. It looked like a lump, but it also looked like a beating heart. There was even a black ball inside the lump that was rolling crazily like an eyeball, releasing intense brainwaves and raising a storm of brainwaves. It was none other than the commander of the Pangu Clan, ‘Dao of Mass Destruction’!

In the middle of the bloody brightness was an extremely ugly member of the Nuwa Clan. Her body was like the amalgamation of a boa and a centipede. Dozens of coldly-shining claws grew out of her ribs and her back. A pair of thin wings grew out of her back, which were covered in blood-red membranes. Even her face looked like a skull that had been drained of all her blood. Driven by a certain crazy force, she was releasing flames of destruction-she was none other than ‘Long Lianzi’, commander of the Nuwa Clan!

After hundreds of thousands of years, when the main body of the Pangu civilization had already vanished into thin air, the Exo, Long Lianzi, and Gu Wuxin were still carrying out their mission dutifully and fighting for the first opportunity to carry out the ‘ultimate test’ of the million-year war!

Chapter 3087: Sea Of Fire Stargate

Judging from the appearance, it was hard to tell whether the enormous object floating in the ocean of magma was a ‘door’ or something else.

It was a square, enormous black cube that was almost a hundred meters long. Complicated stripes and complicated runes were attached to the surface of the cube, which was also covered in rust and mildew. It was obvious that it had been sealed for thousands of years.

Even if such a metal was buried deep inside the magma, it was impossible for it to grow rust and mold.

Therefore, it was very likely that the black cube had been retrieved from another dimension.

As the scorching magma eroded its surface, the rust, mold, and even the shell carved with stripes and rune arrays on the surface of the cube were all peeled off, revealing the pure blackness inside.

When the last shell fragment was gone, what was left in front of everyone was the deepest blackness.

It was like an invisible blade had cut and moved a square piece of space, only to leave an immeasurable hollow inside which there was nothing. No light, waves, or telepathic thoughts could penetrate through it.

Li Yao quickly scanned the shape of the black void and discovered that the ratio of length, width, and height was exactly 9:4:1. It was absolutely precise and did not miss at all.

He had only heard Li Linghai mention similar ‘objects’ before. They were the first gate leading to the central area from the periphery of the primeval relics.

But the scale of the gate and its appearance were hundreds of times more magnificent.

It was especially so when the black rectangular object echoed with the stars in the night sky. Billions of stars turned into gold threads of brightness and jumped into the object one after another, making the black rectangular object as brilliant and splendid as a black crystal that had been embedded with countless stars. The brilliance and majesty of the object fascinated everybody present. On the top of the snow mountain, in the middle of the ocean of magma, space was cracking, and a black gate was flashing. Billions of years and countless stars were all inside!

Li Yao’s soul was deeply attracted by the black rectangular object that was embedded with stars. Distant and vigorous roars echoed in his ears, his brain, and his heart. They were the calls of the primeval era.

As his heart beat faster and faster, the strength in the deepest part of the gene strands was released continuously, encouraging him to jump into the black void filled with stars and look for the ultimate answer. The Cosmos Ring on his index finger was emitting orange brightness, too, which turned into an extremely high temperature in the blink of an eye and almost burnt his finger into ashes.

Before he activated it, a streak of brightness jumped out of the Cosmos Ring automatically. It was the half key that Li Linghai and his foster father had retrieved from the ancient tomb.

The half key was glittering. Every delicate rune array on the surface was changing and turning. Black liquid flowed out of the rune arrays and soon enveloped the key, turning it into a small black ball.

Shua! Shua! Shua!

Li Yao thought of something and immediately realized what was going on. It turned out that a key could not only be used by one tester. All the intelligent creatures in the scanning range of the key, be they carbon-based or energy-based, were qualified to accept the challenge.

On second thought, it was only reasonable.

After all, the ultimate test left by the creator of the black wall was to find the heir or to wait for the prey to walk into the trap.

Whether it was the heir or the prey, naturally, the more the merrier. The larger the range of selection, the more suitable the candidate would be.
