The mental waves of the two parties condensed into visible tides in the air and crashed into each other brutally like a magnificent army.

Li Yao’s eyes, ears, and nose were all soaked in blood. Everything he saw and heard was blurred.

The malformed lumps on the shoulder of the Exo Society shrank rapidly, too. Several veins as thick as earthworms were broken, too. Blood spurted out like arrows.

Both sides were awed by each other’s indomitable fighting will, and the Sect of Destruction was apparently more surprised than Li Yao.

Li Yao had long anticipated the intensity of the mental waves of the strongest expert of the Pangu Clan. But it never occurred to him that the seemingly shameless, sordid human being who had been crawling in front of Long Lianzi a moment ago could be so tough, crazy, and resolute!

BOOM!

It all happened in the blink of an eye. While the members of the Sect of Immortals were slightly surprised, Li Yao, who had accelerated to the highest speed, smashed into the enemy regardless of whether it was 37 or 20.

The curved vibration shield that almost covered half of his body was pushed to the maximum, as if a hundred shield machines had dug in at the same time. Together with the terrifying speed and mass of the solar catastrophe, even the strongest expert of the Pangu Clan such as the Sect of Immortals could not withstand such a brutal collision.

The shield of the calamity of the sun was blown to smithereens. Even the arm that was holding the shield was almost broken into pieces. In return, the Sect of Doom was blown away like a kite whose string had been cut off, buying half a second for Li Yao.

Li Yao had calculated everything perfectly. The Sect of Destruction was blown away to where Long Lianzi was, while he changed his direction and smashed toward the black space again. BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

The power units of the calamity of the sun exploded one after another in the last crazy sprint.

The Giant God Soldier moaned. Every unit was shaking and screaming, ready to be dismantled at any moment. Right now, even the deities and devils wouldn’t be able to stop Li Yao from getting in touch with the black stargate.

There was nothing else in Li Yao’s vision or his brain except for the black stargate that seemed to be a miniature universe with billions of stars.

The Exterminator Sect and Long Lianzi seemed to be roaring in shock and fury behind him. They bombarded the ‘sun catastrophe’ with destructive death rays and ripples one after another. Despite the protection of multiple layers of plate armor, Li Yao felt that his spine was burning in pain, as if his spine had been broken. His limbs were out of his control and paralyzed.

However, it didn’t matter.

As long as he was the first to enter and successfully pass the “Ultimate Test”, nothing else mattered. The moment before the solar catastrophe was about to collapse and Li Yao perished, he finally crashed into the black cube that was embedded with stars.

It was a gamble.

If the black stargate was not a teleportation array as he had expected but a real indestructible black rock, or if he needed something special before he entered the black stargate, he would have smashed the solar catastrophe and himself into pieces with his unbelievable speed without the attack of the Cultivators and Long Lianzi.

It was also the biggest reason why nobody was willing to break into the black stargate recklessly despite the fierce battles just now.

After all, the corpses of a hundred ancient civilizations were right in front of them. The experts of the Pangu Civilization were still somewhat ‘frightened about the tests of the primeval civilization.

Li Yao could not say that he was not scared at all.

But inside his body, there were countless forces that far exceeded his fear.

It was exactly those people who had given him strength that had supported him to this day, and it was also the people who would support him to continue walking forward and to the hopeful tomorrow—he firmly believed in this.

“Come on. Whatever the test is, whatever the legacy or the traps are, I’m ready for them. We human beings will never back off. Come on!”

Li Yao released his body, soul, and soul into the embrace of the black stargate.

“Splash!”

The moment the Giant God Soldier collided with the black stargate, there were no earsplitting collisions or explosions. Instead, there was the sound of a meteorite falling into the ocean.

It was as if Li Yao, riding the calamity of the sun, had truly fallen into a literal sea of stars.

The roars and attacks of the Exterminator Sect and the Dragon Lotus Seed all turned into vague waves that were tossed out of the sky. The snow mountain and the ocean of magma were gone, too. What replaced them was a dazzling ‘darkness’ that was full of stars.

His giant soldier, his crystal suit, and even his body gradually turned transparent and vanished. The only thing that remained was his consciousness that was falling, falling, and falling into the bottomless primeval world.

Along the way, countless shining stars circled around him and turned into vivid and cruel paintings.

In a trance, Li Yao saw that countless war machines that were even larger than the ‘Star Knights’ had been frozen into giant blocks of ice by the overwhelming avalanche.

Another species that was surrounded by orange energy fields that seemed to be burning forever and were even more eye-catching than the Zhurong Clan struggled in the ‘weight ocean’ and tried to surface time and time again, only to run out of energy and be swallowed by the ocean.

There was also an intelligent life form that was translucent and had countless fluorescent tentacles growing on its body. It looked like a giant jellyfish that had evolved into a half-energy form, but it was lost in the “Thunder Valley’. Eventually, it was entangled by electric arcs and melted into ash.

Chapter 3090: The Choice Of Evolution

In addition, there were countless bizarre-shaped, carbon-based, intelligent creatures. Even Li Yao found it hard to tell whether they were carbon-based, silicon-based, or other forms of life. They had all collapsed in different tests.

What left a deep impression on Li Yao was an intelligent life form whose brain had deformed and expanded.

Their body seemed to be only left with an enormous brain, which was much tougher than the brains of the Pangu Clan. But it had been stored in magical equipment that looked like flying saucers and attached to various mechanical arms and plug-in units with the ability to manipulate objects in the air and the spiritual magnetic field. Together, they were a perfect combination of living creatures and machines.

However, such a perfect creation collapsed in front of the black stargate and turned into grey statues.

According to the Observer, there used to be an extremely intelligent species whose level of evolution was among the best of the hundred ancient civilizations. They solved every problem posed by the Forerunners perfectly and passed the ultimate test flawlessly. But in the end, the Forerunners wiped them out mercilessly.

The intelligent species wailed before their extinction, claiming that the Forerunners did not leave any precious legacy and that the place was just a cruel death trap. They also warned the latecomers not to walk into the trap again. It was not hard to guess that the race whose brain had been deformed and whose flesh and blood had completely degenerated was the race that had been replaced by rebar and iron bones. They were the so-called ‘perfect clearers’.

The meaning of the pictures and the information in Li Yao’s head was obvious, too. It was a warning and a threat.

It was as if Teacher Origin was using this method to tell the tester that its secret treasure was not something that could be obtained easily. The price of failure was incomparably miserable. Therefore, it was best for the tester to think clearly in an extremely calm state whether or not it should change its direction and return home.

This was the last chance to give up the test.

Unfortunately, the word ‘give up’ simply did not exist in Li Yao’s dictionary.

Passing through the picture scrolls where countless civilizations had perished, passing through the corpses of civilizations that were smarter, stronger, braver, kinder, or crueler than him, Li Yao was moving faster and faster toward the center of the Dark Abyss. The Giant God Soldier was broken into pieces. The mustard suit and the crystal suit had both been blown to smithereens. The body of flesh and blood had collapsed and vanished. Even the mental devil and Lu Qingchen had been swept away by a tremendous force, making it impossible for Li Yao to reach out to them. It was possible that the ‘ultimate test’ procedure had treated them as independent entities and prepared three tests for Li Yao, the mental devil, and Lu Qingchen.

They had to complete the test alone.

Li Yao was more and more anxious, and his fighting will was firmer, too. The mental devil, Lu Qingchen, the Vanquishing Sect, Long Lianzi, and everybody else could not have been faster than him. He was definitely the first to pass the test!

Finally,

The stars dispersed, and the darkness was shattered. Vague brightness appeared in his vision. Although the brightness was fuzzy and twisted, he could still distinguish the overlapping scenes.

Li Yao was greatly shocked and dumbfounded by what he saw.

Passing through the black stargate, stepping over the corpses of countless civilizations, and returning to the primeval era, he seemed to have fallen into a boiling primitive ocean. The sky was filled with intense dark clouds and irascible lightning. The lightning slashed at the ocean like golden blades, stirring the waves. The plate movement of the undersea was extremely intense. Orange magma and black pillars of smoke were spurting out of the gaps that were torn apart, making the ocean look like a bizarre-shaped ancient creature.

At this moment, the ocean was both quiet and noisy.

The silence was because the first batch of creatures had just been born and had not grasped the ability to swallow and communicate yet.

The noise was because of the undersea volcanic eruptions, the continuous collisions of ocean currents and tides, the never-ending roars of thunder in the sky, and the rise and fall of the continent. All of this had awakened the life force of the ocean in the beginning and presented billions of possibilities for the great explosion of life.

Li Yao found that he had lost his body and turned into an ancient fungus in the ancient ocean.

No. ‘An ancient fungus’ was not a suitable description. In fact, he felt that he had turned into a cluster of ancient fungi of the same nature. A blanket of fungi hundreds of meters in diameter was attached to the undersea volcano and lived on the heat of the magma. But on the other hand, his consciousness was not only concentrated on the creep, but also extended to the bottom of the ocean thousands of kilometers away. He could even cruise in the unpredictable sky with lightning and thunder as if he were watching the evolution of the first living creatures of the world from the perspective of a ‘third party’.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

“Seriously? This again?”

Li Yao was about to be dumbfounded. But right now, not only did he not have eyes and a tongue, even his flagella had not evolved yet. He could only wonder in a daze, Is this the ultimate test? It is like a ‘game’ that is played with the consciousness. Then, how can I pass the test? Right now, I am just a slowly wriggling carpet of fungi. I don’t even have anyone to talk to. Where is Master? What are the rules?

Hardly had the thought occurred to him when a stream of bright gold information flowed out of his vision like a waterfall, providing countless data and options for him.

Li Yao immediately understood.

This was the ‘first question’ of the ultimate test. The tester would have to manipulate the primitive creepers to repeat the path of evolution and evolve into a carbon-based intelligent life form that could communicate with the Primeval Lord of Heaven in order to receive the legacy of the Primeval Lord of Heaven.

Every ancient fungus inside the blanket was growing, splitting, and dying. In the process of division and death, the odds of mutations were slim. The tester had to choose the ‘right’ mutations and eliminate the ‘failed’ mutations in order to make the entire species stronger and ignite the fire of wisdom.

Of course, different testers must have different understandings about what was ‘correct’ and ‘mistake’.

A tiny error could lead to a huge error. When a life was born in the beginning, every tiny mutation would determine how weird, wise, and tough the intelligent life was.

Li Yao had no time to think. The direction of billions of mutations exploded in his vision.

The ultimate test could condense the river of evolution for billions of years in the blink of an eye. Therefore, countless variations were waiting for Li Yao’s choice every second.

Some mutations could increase the longevity of the ancient fungi, but at the cost of a slower division speed.

Some mutations went in the opposite direction. The longevity of the ancient fungi was shortened by half, but the speed of their division was 50%. Some of the mutations could turn the archaea into spherical, helical, rod-shaped, disc-shaped, and irregular forms. Some other mutations could evolve the archaea into Li Yao’s favorite flagella, thereby increasing their mobility and ability to swallow food.

Some of the mutations could alter the structure of the outer membrane of the cells of the archaea, making them more suitable for the environment near undersea volcanoes. But there were also other changes that could improve the toughness of the flagella and the overall hardness of the creep, so that the creep would be far away from the undersea volcanoes and march toward the vast undersea.

Every second, hundreds of bright gold options were floating in front of Li Yao’s eyes. Most of the time, he did not even have the time to think carefully and could only select and choose based on his intuition.

Li Yao filtered the direction of the mutations that could prolong the life of the ancient fungi out of instinct, but he kept the ability of ‘more splits’.

Only by splitting more times could there be more possibilities for evolution. Now was not the time to talk about longevity and stability.

Then, he ruled out the mutation that could change the structure of the cell membrane and absorb more heat from the magma.

Li Yao was very clear that life was inert. If the archaea evolved into structures that were very suitable for undersea volcanoes, they would stay in this place forever and maintain a simple, low-level structure. It would be impossible for them to march into the vast ocean and evolve into more, more complicated, and more advanced structures.

Born in peace, die in sorrow. It was the same for any life. Too much adaptation to the environment was the ultimate bliss. Only by marching forward, fighting against the environment, fighting against the heaven and the earth, and marching toward the cruel unknown was the true meaning of life!

At this moment, a new direction of mutation flashed before Li Yao’s eyes.

Li Yao’s consciousness shrank to the size of a needle tip. He grasped the option at lightning speed.

This was it!

After prolonged competition and evolution, one of his fungi finally mutated into the ability to carry genetic information with ‘deoxyribonucleic acid’!

Before that, all the ancient fungi stored and transmitted genetic information with ‘ribonucleic acid’. Li Yao was very clear that, although ‘ribonucleic acid’ was a long chain molecule that was very suitable to carry information, the amount of information stored and the stability of it were far less than that of ‘deoxyribonucleic acid’!

Only after the evolution of deoxyribonucleic acid’ could the capacity of the genetic information be exponentially expanded, thereby making a leap from ‘ancient life’ to ‘ancient life!

BOOM!

After he chose ‘deoxyribonucleic acid’ to replace most of the functions of ‘ribonucleic acid’, the entire primeval ocean was swallowed by the golden light ball, as if an earthshaking explosion had taken place.

Then, the environment was completely different. The eruptions of the undersea volcanoes gradually died down, and the crust and weather became relatively stable. More and more ancient creatures of the ocean evolved, too. The high-level, multi-cellular creatures that looked like trilobites, nautilus, and armored fish were swimming among the sea anemones, seaweeds, and colorful jellyfish. Li Yao was faced with a life-and-death choice again.

The golden information stream told him that, according to the mutated genes that he had right now, he could choose to evolve into a trilobite, a nautilus, an armored fish, and other top predators of the ancient ocean. Of course, he could also choose to become a second-level predator and receive compensation in other directions of evolution.

Chapter 3091: The Birth Of All Creatures!

The trilobites, the Nautilus, and the Armored Fish were all out-and-out dominators of the

ocean.

The trilobites and the Nautilus both boasted hard shells and were relatively ‘huge’. They were the most terrifying killing machines in an era when most of the ancient ocean creatures were no larger than plankton.

There was no need to mention the Armored Fish. Other than its hard shell, it also possessed a swift movement ability, allowing it to charge and sweep away all prey. But Li Yao eliminated the three seemingly tempting evolution options without any hesitation.

Because he was very clear that no matter how powerful the trilobites, the Nautilus, and the Armored Fish appeared to be, in the end, they were eliminated in the long river of time.

Their enormous size meant that they had to consume more energy in order to survive. Most of their time was spent on hunting and absorbing.

Their overly hard shells, on the one hand, hindered their ability to mutate further, and on the other hand, they indulged in the illusionary sense of security and lost the need for evolution.

A life without a sense of crisis and ambition was destined to be a life without a future. It was impossible to ignite the fire of evolution. It would only become a fossil billions of years later.

Therefore, Li Yao eliminated all the top predators and chose an inconspicuous fish as the carrier of his consciousness.

Although they were little silver fish, their average size was no more than half a centimeter. They were like glittering silver bugs.

The small silver fish barely had any hunting ability. On the contrary, they were the best food for the top predators such as the Nautilus, trilobites, and Armored Fish. When the top predators were wandering around them, they had no ability to resist and could only wait to be slaughtered.

But their biggest advantage was their unparalleled reproductive and survival ability.

Their life cycles were extremely short, and they had been mating and reproducing almost every second of their short lives. They had already evolved into the primary sexes. The female fish could shoot out thousands of life seeds that looked like dust after each spurt. As long as they absorbed the heat near the surface of the ocean, they would have a chance to survive. Their unique digestive system could transform almost all food into energy. From the plankton on the surface of the ocean to the carrion of the top predators, from the primitive fungi in the bottom of the ocean to the seaweed and the sea anemones, they could even attach themselves to the ancient jellyfish and form a harmonious symbiotic body with them, surviving on the residues of the food that the jellyfish digested.

At the beginning of life, quantity was far more important than quality.

Li Yao’s consciousness was devoted to the silver fish. He was also trying his best to select the mutations that could increase the number of his species.

He refused to expand the size of the small silver fish and ruled out the possibility of covering the surface of the small silver fish with a solid shell. Of course, he did not hesitate to wipe out the seemingly aggressive mutations such as the growing of sharper teeth, which were in fact self-destruction. In the end, he gave the small silver fish a stronger digestive and reproductive ability. Of course, there was also the subtle ability to perceive the magnetic field of a planet as well as a stronger ability to travel

Just like that, after billions of years of evolution, the little silver fish and their different mutations gradually spread to the entire ocean. From the cold, dark North Pole to the sunny equator, the shadows of the little silver fish were everywhere.

In the meantime, the trilobites were covered by the mud on the bottom of the ocean, and the Nautilus became empty. The Armored Fish, which used to be the best fish in the world, were gradually burdened by their heavy ‘armor, shields, and blades’ after they evolved into sturdier shells, sharper teeth, and larger and larger bodies. They could not keep up with the agility and speed of the new generation of ocean creatures and were eventually devoured by their insatiable appetite, turning into the losers of evolution.

A new dominator of the ocean had appeared.

They were either streamlined, fast, or agile sharks.

Some of them were octopuses that could freely change the color and size of their skin, grow eight tentacles that were both hard and soft, and could even increase their speed by spraying water.

There were even jellyfish that had evolved into venomous ones.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Most of the dominators of the ocean considered the silver fish as one of the main dishes on their menu.

There was a kind of whale with star spots all over its body that could swallow several tons of silver fish in one breath. For this, it had evolved a set of organs that could filter seawater and absorb the silver fish.

However, no matter how much they slaughtered, they could not reduce the overall number of the little silver fish. Instead, the little silver fish became the foundation of the entire food chain, the key to the survival of countless species.

Li Yao vaguely felt that the time was right.

March! March! He had to march to the land without caring about anything!

Although the ocean was vast, except for the places near the continental shelf and undersea volcanoes, the resources were not as abundant as one might expect. On the contrary, they were as barren as a desert filled with distilled water.

More importantly, the pressure of the seawater made it difficult for natural catalysts such as ‘fire’ to exist, not to mention the refinement of metals and the development of civilization. Most of the ancestors of the intelligent carbon-based creatures were born in the ocean, but they had to experience the ‘dangerous jump’ from the ocean to the land. Otherwise, it would be very difficult to develop a mature and efficient civilization.

Although the universe was vast and full of wonders, and there were many intelligent creatures such as the ‘Gonggong Clan’ that were born in rivers, lakes, and oceans like jellyfish, they were still the minority in the end. Even the Gonggong Clan did not really evolve into the space era until they received the help of the Pangu Clan and other primeval species. Under Li Yao’s command, a magnificent, soul-stirring ‘landing battle’ from the ocean to the land began.

This was not a war that could be fought overnight, nor was it a war for the gains and losses of a city. It was a war across billions of years for the rise or death of a civilization.

The little silver fish that was washed to the beach by the tides accidentally or jumped to the beach in a panic to avoid the top predators failed as expected.

But Li Yao had enough patience and a clear direction of evolution. He grasped the most useful possibilities of billions of mutations and granted part of the silver fish the ability to breathe in the air as well as the ability to jump and dig on the beach with their thick and powerful fins.

The silver fish had even learned the ability to dig holes on the beach with their fins, crawl into the holes, and keep themselves wet while they waited for the next tide so that they could move freely in the ocean and on land. They were like some kind of ancient creature that could play with mud.

The road of evolution was destined to be lonely and dangerous.

The little silver fish that were jumping on land encountered hundreds of times more difficulties and dangers than they did in their warm and comfortable hometown, the ocean. However, in order to resolve the dangers, or rather, in the cruel competition for survival, more genes that were not adapted to the changes were eliminated, which endowed generations of little silver fish with a stronger survival ability.

The scales and fins were gone, replaced by stronger limbs and tails. Their skin was covered in air-proof mucus. The first row of sharp teeth grew out of their mouths, indicating that they had evolved from fish into amphibians.

For the first time, the little silver fish breathed in the dry air, saw the volcanic eruptions, the rolling mountains and rivers, and saw the lush primitive forest.

The path of evolution after that was like walking on the fast track. Everything was a hundred times faster than before.

Times were changing. The environment was changing. All the creatures were changing crazily like clockwork.

Li Yao refused to let the species under his control move toward the giant crustaceans, because he knew that, although giant insects and arthropods were the dominators of the land in ancient times, as the plants mutated and the oxygen content in the air plummeted, the size of the giant insects and the arthropods would be reduced, and they would be eliminated from the competition of the top predators.

He also rejected the idea of turning into a ‘civilization of insects’ that resembled bees. Although it was possible that a strong civilization could be born out of the collective wisdom, it did not fit Li Yao’s ideology after all.

Similarly, he rejected the path of the giant reptiles without even thinking. When dinosaurs were stomping on the ground, slaughtering, and roaring, he would rather shrink the size of the creatures under his command and flee among the bushes and caves.

Mammals were the only right path of evolution. Li Yao believed in that and bore the price that he had to pay.

As it turned out, the meteoroid shower that he had been expecting came one after another. The mountains and the rivers lost their color, the sun and the moon lost their brightness, and countless plants withered and perished. More and more new plants changed the environment in a completely different way. The ‘king of the land’ dinosaurs who used to flaunt their strength were either killed by the raging flames raised by the meteoroid shower, or by the drastic changes in the composition of the air, or the plants that could not digest the new plants. They could only watch the lush green plants starve to death.

This was a new era, a stage for mammals!

At this moment, Li Yao’s cautious choices in the beginning and his determination to hold on to his life were both greatly rewarded. The seemingly useless genes were all displaying colorful mutations, allowing him to dominate the brand-new biological explosion. He was the first to successfully evolve into a large enough brain with enough synapses as well as a body of flesh and blood that had a perfect balance in terms of strength, agility, and energy consumption. He also unlocked the abilities of “walking upright, freeing hands”, “using flames, creating tools”, and so on. It could be said to be an “epoch-making” ability.

Mammoths, saber-toothed tigers, and all kinds of seemingly powerful wolves, tigers, and leopards fell to the attacks of stone axes and spears one after another. Li Yao’s tribe wreaked havoc on the land and expanded to even farther places. When the tribe first learned how to set fire to mountains, drive out and burn down ferocious beasts, the raging fire represented the birth of the real dominator, the spirit of all creatures who would eventually rule the universe!

Chapter 3092: Yuanshi Clan

It was a vast lake.

It was round, the water of the lake was pure, and it looked like a silver-white round mirror at the foot of the rolling mountains.

Once upon a time, this place was a place where countless living creatures gathered to drink water. It was also a hunting ground for ferocious beasts such as wolves, tigers, and leopards.

However, since the rise of mankind and the occupation of the mirror-like lake, no ferocious beasts dared to hunt in this place. The ferocious beasts that were too stupid to realize the horror of human beings had all turned into piles of bones by the lake, towers and tombstones of different shapes and sizes.

That night, the stars were particularly brilliant. Even the big, round moon could not cover the brilliance of the stars. The stars congregated into a vast ocean, like a silver storm that was raining down from the sky.

A bonfire had been lit in the tribe at the foot of the mountain. They were celebrating the hunting of another enormous prey. A tribesman, who had wrinkles all over his face and a hunched back, somehow left his tribesmen and went to the lake in high and low steps.

Behind them was a joyous and raucous celebration.

In front of him was a silent and deep unknown.

The tribesmen stared at their own reflection in the lake at first.

His forehead was slightly tilted back, and his nose was large and flat. The hair on his face had not entirely faded away, but two enormous canine teeth were protruding out of his lips. At first glance, he was still unable to get rid of the traces of antiquity and ignorance.

But his dirty eyes, which were slightly yellow, were absorbing the reflection of the lake water quietly. They gradually turned deep and sharp, adding darkness and silver.

The eyes of the tribe members gradually turned sharp. They licked the moon in the lake first, before they extended to the sky along the moonlight and the stars.

When billions of stars exploded in front of his eyes, he could not help but extend his hairy arms toward the endless sea of stars, as if he were trying to hold the entire universe in his hands.

“Hoho, hohoho!”

He began to howl, as if he were trying to break the darkness of the universe with his voice and give a name to the moon and every star. He also summoned his compatriots to the moon and the stars one day and see what the world in the sky looked like.

BOOM!

The moment the tribesmen extended their hands toward the starry sky and screamed, dazzling gold brightness burst out again, indicating that the tester had completed the second leap and evolved into the middle ages. A real ‘civilization’ had been born!

Li Yao watched everything quietly.

Like an omnipresent, omnipotent god, he watched the rise, struggle, and conquest of countless human tribes from above.

Then, he crawled into the body of the tribesman who was looking at the starry sky in front of the lake and sensed the indescribable joy and gratitude when human beings got to know him for the first time.

“That’s it?”

Watching ‘himself’ dancing in the lake, Li Yao mumbled in confusion, “Is this the so-called ‘ultimate test’?”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

No wonder he was confused.

Although it was very difficult to make thousands of choices for the evolution of life and eventually the birth of a civilization in such a short period of time, the choices did not escape the basic route of human evolution. Li Yao only needed to make the choices based on his natural instincts, and he had basically completed the advancement from an ancient fungus carpet to a human tribe. Such a test did not seem to be compatible with the strength and mystery of the creator of the black wall, nor was it enough to stump a hundred ancient civilizations!

Li Yao pondered for a moment and realized that he had taken it for granted.

From his point of view, he naturally felt that the ‘ultimate test’ was too easy, because he was a pure human being and a clone of 100% of the original Master’s genes.

Therefore, he was very clear about the path of evolution that he, the Master, had taken. His instinctive reaction was the correct answer.

But what if the tester was not a human being like him but an intelligent life form with a carbon-based body?

For example, if the testers were born in rivers, lakes, and oceans like jellyfish and sponges, would they be able to tell that the ‘little silver fish’ were the species most likely to climb to the peak of evolution?

Or rather, would a test tester of the ‘Gonggong Clan’ choose to go to the land instead of exploring the ocean in search of the one billionth likelihood that a civilization would be born in the seawater?

By the same logic, the ‘Zhurong Clan’ was an intelligent species that was born in volcanoes and magma and lived on the high temperature and the ‘poisonous gas’. When a test subject of the Zhhurong Clan was in the undersea volcanic environment in the beginning, could he really resist the temptation of altering the structure of the membrane and absorbing the heat?

It was the same for the ‘Pangu’, the most successful, most powerful, carbon-based intelligent life form. Compared to human beings, the Pangu Clan was closer to the giant reptiles. Many of the Pangu Clan members looked like tyrannosaurs in human form. Perhaps, on the original planet of the Pangu Clan, the giant reptiles such as dinosaurs had never encountered the scourge of meteoroids or the abrupt changes of climate, which resulted in the unique appearance of the Pangu Clan today.

Then, when a candidate of the Pangu Clan was faced with a choice between a reptile and a mammal, could he choose a mammal as naturally as Li Yao did and persist through all the difficulties and setbacks? It was impossible. The logical thinking of every kind of carbon-based intelligent life was greatly influenced by the form of their body, which was the environment of the original planet. Even though they had retrieved the cells of the Master and analyzed the gene segments of the Master, which gave them a general idea of how the Master evolved, they could not help but be marked by their own civilization when they had to make hundreds of choices in the ever-changing environment.

In other words, the mutations of the Gonggong Clan, the Zhurong Clan, the Pangu Clan, or the ‘mechanical civilization’, the ‘black iron civilization’, the ‘half-energy civilization’, and so on were definitely not what Li Yao was seeing right now.

From this point of view, the experts and scholars of the Heavenly Palace were on the right track. Only by replicating a real human being could they completely see through the thoughts of the Teacher and even complete the test of the Teacher without any trouble.

However, this was just the beginning.

From the bottom of the ocean to the land, from ignorance to wisdom, from the spirits of all creatures to the king of the universe, mankind still had a long way to go. The ‘path to heaven’ that led to the universe along the stars was probably the real test. As he expected, when the thought flashed in his mind, another dull, deep, and ancient voice echoed.

“You’re here.”

The voice said.

The way the voice communicated with information was similar to that of the Pangu Clan and the Nuwa Clan, except that it employed a very special structure of information compression and encryption. “In order to produce all the factors, there is a fundamental premise, which is the relative equality between people.

When the starships that we built with thousands of turrets tore apart their atmosphere, they were still waving the hilarious blades and using the shabby spiritual energy, trying to compete with us! “Moreover, in the most extreme spiritual energy civilization, the strongest expert absorbed the spiritual energy of an entire planet into his body, turning himself into an old monster who could live for tens of thousands of years, while his other companions turned into parasites on his body. They were completely unable to resist him. However, because there was absolutely nothing in the entire environment that could threaten him, his intelligence gradually declined, and he became an idiot that was even worse than a three-year-old. He was easily captured by us and treated as an exhibit in the zoo.

“We have slowly grasped our experience. Before we wage a war against an unknown civilization, we must first figure out the situation of the mother planet from which they originated. If it is a planet with abundant spiritual energy and resources, there is no need to worry. It will certainly be an unstoppable victory. But if the environment of the enemy’s mother planet is extremely harsh, the resources are scarce, and there is not even a tiny bit of spiritual energy, we must be extremely careful. It will certainly be a tough battle.

Chapter 3096: World War

Li Yao nodded in silence.

In fact, the logic that the voice spoke of was reflected in the history of human civilization in the Pangu Universe, too. It was the chaos of the ancient Cultivation world and the tragedy of the ‘living fossil’.

No power could be achieved without paying any price. Fate had marked the price for every kind of power. The power that seemed easy to acquire might have to be redeemed by something more precious-organization, social order, exploration, and curiosity.

Thankfully, the human civilization of the Pangu Universe, including the Pangu civilization before the human civilization and the hundred ancient civilizations before the Pangu civilization, was not strictly a first-generation civilization but a descendant of the Primeval Civilization.

On one hand, they could find the relics of the previous civilization deep below the ground and in the vast sea of stars, where they could learn valuable lessons from them and avoid repeating the mistakes. On the other hand, the primeval memories from the primeval race were buried deep inside their genes. Although the ancient memories were mottled and fuzzy most of the time, they were enough to stimulate the instincts of the intelligent creatures and form complicated social and highly-organized structures.

Therefore, although the ‘second generation civilizations’ were able to develop a relatively strong civilization in an environment where the spiritual gas was abundant, there were bound to be a lot of detours. For example, the collapse of the ancient Cultivators and the Great Dark Age of 30,000 years, as well as the evil deeds of the Holy Accord Alliance and the Imperium of True Human Beings today, were partly because of the influence of ‘the strong are too strong while the weak are too weak’.

The native civilizations that did not have the relics of the previous civilizations nor did they have the ‘organizational instincts’ buried deep inside their genes suddenly had inexhaustible spiritual energy. They were like seven-year-olds who had received a huge legacy but did not have the education of adults. They could only squander whatever they wanted. There was barely anyone who was not corrupted. “Of course, spiritual energy is one of the most precious resources in the universe. But only a civilization that is highly mature and has its own belief is qualified to drive it instead of being enslaved by it. The voice continued, “It is precisely because we don’t have the help of spiritual energy that we can stimulate all our passion, wisdom, and courage. With our hands, sweat, and blood, we can modify our planet and get to know the universe outside of it.

“We went through the first industrial revolution, the second, and the third.

“We have discovered the patterns of the celestial bodies. We have also discovered molecules, atoms, and structures that are smaller than atoms.

“We have discovered electromagnetic waves, and we know how to make use of radio. We have conquered mountains and seas, and we have conquered countless kinds of bacteria. We even know how to drive mold to treat fatal diseases. We believe in technology more sincerely than we believe in gods. We believe that the path of technology is infinite, and the infinite technology will eventually make us the gods of ourselves and the entire universe. “Of course, the advancement of technology and the sudden expansion of civilization led to crazy ambitions and bloody slaughters. In the first fifty years of the twentieth century, we launched two world wars one after another. If the main battlefield of the first world war was limited to one continent, the deadly storm of the second world war swept across the entire mother planet.

“Those two wars were both glorious and cruel. The people, who lacked spiritual energy and had no strength to even truss a chicken, were able to unleash damage that was even more terrifying than that of the Cultivators. Countless tons of steel and explosives rained down upon the city, which had a population of hundreds of thousands.

“At the end of the Second World War, we employed a weapon named ‘Atomic Bomb’, which involved the mysteries of material annihilation and energy outbursts. It was even more terrifying than the main gun of a starship on a planet with an atmosphere. “However, wars, on one hand, are the devils who destroy civilizations, and on the other hand, they are catalysts for the rapid development of civilizations. The new technologies and ideas born in the two world wars, including the new system born on countless old systems that have been destroyed, have boosted the explosive development of our civilization in the last fifty years of the twentieth century and pushed it forward at a lightning fast speed.

“After the end of the Second World War, more than twenty years later, on July 21, 1969, our primitive and simple starship boarded the only satellite of our mother planet for the first time and took the first step to conquer the sea of stars.

Along with the voice, a new picture appeared in Li Yao’s mind.

Three obese and clumsy astronauts wearing white spacesuits and star-striped flags were bouncing on the surface of the moon. In the low and noisy background, the voice of one of them could be heard.

“… This is a small step for me, but it is a big step for mankind.”

Of course, the astronaut was speaking in English, which was the ‘magical language’ in Li Yao’s understanding.

“The brutal destruction of the Second World War and the cold war that resulted from the confrontation of the two superpowers not only gave birth to space technology and the study of the universe that far exceeded the limits of our civilization, but also woke up the ancient people who had the largest population in our civilization. The ancient civilization that stood in the east was revived after hundreds of years of silence. It was full of vigor and creativity.

The voice said, “The last 50 years of the 20th century to the first 20 years of the 21st century is almost the golden era of the fastest development of our civilization. New technologies and research results are released almost every year. Every ten years, the entire civilization will look brand new. In 1950, the electronic computer was still a fantasy for most people, but after 60-70 years, it can be worn on people’s wrists and become an essential production tool and life partner for everyone. People are even starting to imagine the birth of artificial intelligence in the next hundred years. “However, the beautiful dream suddenly broke like a golden soap bubble. The big bang of our technology hit the physical limit and forced us to step on the brakes. In the third decade of the 21st century, we encountered stagnation and setbacks in various fields and barely made any valuable discoveries.

“With the stagnation of technology, a series of diseases and fatal defects burst out of the body of our civilization.

“The population explosion brought by the advanced medical technology, on the other hand, lacked a good education system and enough jobs. The vast majority of the population was reduced to an ‘invalid population’ or even an ‘invalid population’. They became a burden for the civilization.

Chapter 3097: The Supernatural Era

The picture of a meteoroid hitting the earth billions of years ago appeared in Li Yao’s brain.

At that time, after thirty years of thermonuclear war and the scourge of biochemical weapons, Earth had become a rusted wasteland.

As far as one’s eyes could see, the earth was covered in a layer of dust that looked like grey snow. The broken walls of the skyscrapers were like decayed corpses, and the deformed people living in twisted rebar and concrete were more like maggots on a rotten corpse than intelligent creatures.

Against such a background, when the splendid, glittering meteoroid tore through the atmosphere and crashed into the ground, it was more like ‘purification’ and ‘rebirth’ than ‘destruction’.

BOOM! BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM!

Under the super high-speed friction of the atmosphere, the meteoroid suddenly split apart, revealing its translucent core, which was then broken into thousands of pieces like a storm of crystals that splashed on the land that had been exposed to radiation. Countless craters dozens of kilometers in diameter were created in the extremely twisted towns and empty military bases, and the dust waves that it raised could spread hundreds of kilometers away.

“The broken pieces of the meteoroids are three to five meters in diameter. The explosion of each piece is like the explosion of a small-yield nuclear weapon, which has dealt a great deal of damage to our planet. Thankfully, in order to avoid the radiation and the contamination of the virus, most of the survivors have fled to the underground shelters. The only survivors who are still living on the ground are the deformed, mutated ‘radiators’ and the ‘modified humans’. In other words, Earth has already been wrecked by us. What’s the big deal about a few more meteoroids?”

The voice said, “At first, the news of the meteorite did not attract the attention of the authorities, because the control over the major countries on Earth had been greatly undermined. They could only control a few large underground shelters and military bases effectively and had only a few elite troops. They were also wary of each other’s assaults. It was difficult to investigate the causes and consequences of the meteorite.

“However, the falling meteoroids soon presented bizarre, unbelievable miracles, indicating that they might be the only hope to save the civilization of mankind.

The picture in Li Yao’s brain jumped to one year after the meteorite crash.

At this moment, the dust that was filled with the debris of the meteoroids had filled the hundreds of square kilometers around the point of impact. In the area, all kinds of crops and creatures that had been savaged or even gone extinct were revived. From grass to trees, from bushes to vines, from mice and rabbits to animals such as wolves, tigers, and leopards, they all reappeared and were even more agile and stronger than before.

The wasteland, which had been barren a year ago, had turned into a thriving forest.

W

wei

Deep inside the forest, a ‘radiator’ whose skin was full of pustules, whose lips were split into four halves, and whose teeth were sharp, was marching forward quickly with a saber in his hand and a camouflage suit.

He seemed to regard the front as his only hope. No matter how terrifying the screams of the animals around him were, he did not stop and simply stumbled into the giant crater and crawled to the front of the meteoroid.

What a magnificent meteorite it was!

It was like an enormous, glittering diamond. Bright red brilliance was beaming out of its body. Even though the scorching sun above his head was covered by the trees, the only thing that could be seen was the vague sunlight. After being refracted by it, everything within hundreds of square meters was drowned in an invisible ocean of flames.

When human beings focused their attention on the depths of the crystal suit, they seemed to be able to see countless mysterious patterns and stripes, which were natural models of spiritual energy constraints and magnetic fields. They were the complicated rules of energy utilization!

“Crystal!”

Li Yao’s soul suddenly constricted. He recognized at a glance that the meteoroids that had fallen to the ancient Earth were the necessary crystals for the world of Cultivators!

But it was enough to bring new changes and hope to a world that had never experienced spiritual energy before.

The man with cracked lips was kneeling on the ground, staring at the crimson crystal shard in front of him.

His expression was somewhat absent-minded and fearful, but it was also filled with reckless courage and hope.

Trembling, he extended his hand toward the crystal shard.

It was not until this moment that Li Yao discovered that his right hand was covered in burning wounds. One of the pustules was folded into a pustule, from which yellow, yellow, and green pus was flowing out. It looked both disgusting and pitiful.

“Radiation disease!”

Li Yao knew that the poor ‘Radiator’ had been contaminated by excessive radiation. The gene strands inside his body had been broken, and part of his limbs had lost their metabolism.

Normal people would have to replace all the cells inside their body every seven days through metabolism. In other words, most of the cells inside the body only had seven days of

life.

As for the people who had been contaminated by radiation, whose gene strands had been broken, or who had undergone high-level mutations, their old cells died, but they were unable to produce new cells. They could only watch their bodies decay bit by bit, turning them into miserable-looking ‘living dead’.

No wonder the Radiator had rushed into the forest full of ferocious beasts and mutated plants without bothering about anything else. He was looking for his last chance of survival.

The Radiator hesitated for a long time in front of the crystal shards, before he finally made up his mind.

He suddenly raised the machete in his left hand and slashed at his right hand.

It was not hard to tell that the Radiator had practiced a saber art that was as fast as lightning in the harsh wasteland. The slash almost cut off all the rotten meat on his right hand, revealing the white bones, but not a single nerve was damaged.

He screamed in pain, but he held back the excruciating pain and moved his bleeding right hand to the crystal shard. He even covered his wounds with the soil that was emitting redness near the crystal shard.

An unbelievable scene took place!

WO

When the redness flowed among the wounds, new meat sprouts grew out of the seemingly hopeless wounds and tangled together at a visible speed, forming a ‘new type of muscle’ that was much tougher and stronger than the normal muscle fibers.

There was no telling how long the entire process took. The blast of spiritual energy and the recovery of his wounds finally exhausted the Radiator, who collapsed to the ground in a coma.

When he was woken up by the heavy rain, his arm was as good as new. Red stripes had grown on his wrist from the previous wounds, making it look like a delicate tattoo of a raging fire.

The Radiator was dizzy. Before he realized what was going on, he had already sensed the danger.

Huala! Huala!

The bushes nearby were shaking violently. A crocodile almost five meters long suddenly dashed out and lunged at the Radiator.

“Ah!”

Right now, the stomach of the Radiator was empty, and his limbs were too weak to dodge or run away. He could only close his eyes and wait to be killed.

Before his death, he waved his hand out of instinct. The crimson stripes on his wrist suddenly burst out dazzling brilliance and formed a sharp blade of light on the edge of his palm, which cut the crocodile’s neck precisely without any hinderance!

The giant head was rolling on the ground. The body that had lost its head was struggling hard, but the wounds were as smooth as a mirror, and the high temperature had sealed its veins. No matter how hard it struggled, no blood spurted out, making the scene look like an absurd dream.

Half a minute later, the crocodile stopped twitching

Breathing heavily, the Radiator stared at the dead body of the crocodile and his burning right hand.

The confusion in his eyes gradually turned into brutality, and the astonishment on his face turned into hideousness. He suddenly grabbed the tough body of the crocodile and started tearing and swallowing it. “Is this… the picture where the Primeval Clan in the primeval era-earthlings—first come into contact with spiritual energy?”.

Li Yao found it hard to believe. “He grasped the utilization of spiritual energy and learned ‘training’ so easily?”

On second thought, it was not absurd.

Firstly, it was just a brief introduction to explain the origin of the Primeval Civilization. In the real history, the ancient human beings had grasped the secret arts of spiritual energy and Cultivation. Of course, it was not that easy.

Secondly, the ancient human beings at that time had just experienced a protracted thermonuclear war and the subsequent biochemical, virus, and genetic wars. Most of the survivors’ bodies were highly mutated, and their gene strands were extremely unstable. They were more likely to be affected by the radiation and the abnormal magnetic field than the healthy human beings in the future.

As he expected, the voice continued. “The thermonuclear war is a catastrophe for our civilization. Meteorite attacks are a catastrophe for our civilization, too. If either of the two catastrophes were to arrive alone, it is very likely that our civilization would be wiped out. However, when the two catastrophes arrived one after another and overlapped, they brought new hope to our civilization and even opened the road to the sea of stars. The evolution of life and civilization is indeed marvelous. Maybe ‘luck’ or ‘fortune’ is the most important thing.

“The thermonuclear war and the biochemical weapons changed our genes forever and shook the indestructible gene strands that had been frozen for billions of years. “In the early years of the Supernova Era, the Radiators and the modified people living on the ground came into contact with the meteorite fragments everywhere. 99% of them could not withstand the spiritual energy or accelerated their genetic mutations, turning them into monsters that would never appear in their nightmares.

“But 1% of the lucky winners corrected the errors of the gene strands through the infiltration of spiritual energy. Not only did they become healthier, stronger, and faster, they even evolved into all kinds of incredible abilities.

Chapter 3098: Marching Towards The Stars!

It is also difficult for us to create a fertile, habitable planet.

“Huh?”

mol

Li Yao did not realize what was going on for a moment. He thought quickly and shouted, “That’s—that’s absurd! I’m just an adventurer. I’m not a qualified politician or… a scientist at all. How can I be stronger than the billions of politicians and scientists of the Earth Era and make every correct choice in such a short amount of time? Isn’t it too unreasonable?”

“This is just a test. All the options are highly abstract and simplified. Of course, you are not required to know all the details of every technology.

The voice said, “We just want to make sure that your brand-new species, with keen technological insight and future perception, will not waste our heritage. Besides, you have three opportunities to make mistakes. As long as you listen to our suggestions obediently, the test will continue.

“Well—”

Li Yao still found it absurd. “Are all the adventurers of the hundreds of civilizations in the past going through such tests? No wonder they failed the test. It was simply a game, if not based on intuition and luck!”

“As I said just now, ‘luck’ and ‘fortune’ are perhaps the most crucial factors for the rise of a civilization.

The voice said, “It is not wrong to consider the test as a test of your luck.

“But,”

Li Yao was going to argue, but an astronomical amount of information flooded into his brain unstoppably like the terrifying volcanic eruption and avalanche not long ago.

The brand-new ‘passages’ were similar to the peak of carbon-based intelligent life through the manipulation of an ancient fungus and a spore in the beginning. The only difference was that countless ‘technology’ and ‘policy’ replaced the original direction of mutation. Every piece of technology and policy had mutual checks and effects, which could accelerate, unlock, or hinder the implementation of new technologies and policies. The overall complexity was hundreds of times higher than before. In the range of Li Yao’s vision, there was a glittering, blood-red countdown in the upper left corner. It was counting down rapidly from a hundred years ago. Every day seemed as short as a second.

In the upper right corner was a progress sheet, indicating that he had made 1% progress. He was still short of hundreds of key technologies to send human beings out of the solar system and cruise in the sea of stars for tens of thousands of years.

Various technologies, including the parameters of the beta version of Earth, such as the population, the energy status, the pollution index, the latest movements of the major forces, the citizens’ reactions to the ‘apocalypse’, and so on, were presented in the most chaotic and furious data.

As the endless options poured down like a waterfall, Li Yao didn’t even have the time to curse.

‘Hydraulic fracturing mining method’, ‘strengthened exoskeleton’, ‘Nano-level composite armor’, ‘ion thrusters’, ‘outer space ecological circulation system’…

Li Yao had to set the funding, personnel, facilities, and other resources for the development of the technology. He even had to veto the unnecessary technologies in order to determine the completion time of the technology.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

‘Earth Core Mining’, ‘Self-discipline Robots’, ‘Super Logic Computers’…

In the meantime, he had to make breakthroughs in a series of advanced technologies.

Armored torpedo, nuclear fusion missile, pulse gun…

The development of such cutting-edge weapons seemed unnecessary, but who knew whether or not he would run into ancient alien species during his millions of years of wandering in the sea of stars? It was impossible for him to abandon the technological advancements in the field of weapon development.

Even though Li Yao was an outstanding Master Artificer in the era of 40,000 years of Cultivation, it did not mean that he could grasp the correct direction of technological advancement in a world without spiritual energy. The few meteorite fragments brought too few crystals. When applied to the escape of an entire civilization, they were simply like a drop of water in a bucket. Most of the starships and hibernation facilities of the escape fleet had to be resolved by conventional technology. Li Yao was not an expert in such matters at all. Just as the voice had said, most of the time, he could only rely on his ‘intuition’ and ‘luck’ to go on a rampage. On the other hand, it was different from the spore evolution procedure just now. Although he was the leader of the civilization right now, it did not mean that everybody would obey his command.

As the tests went on, Li Yao learned more and more of the hidden parameters of the Earth at that time, which gave him a deeper understanding about the complicated, unpredictable structure of the planet before the apocalypse.

Although the Earth Council became the nominal governing body of the entire civilization, the remnants of the old countries and the people did not disappear immediately. They went underground and were still active as fundamentalists and extremists.

“Those who are not of our species must have different intentions. Only we, the so-and-so people, are the representatives of the entire civilization. Only we are qualified to survive in the universe!

Such arguments were everywhere on Earth at that time.

Moreover, the technology of starship manufacturing and the hibernation of human beings had just begun. Ambitionists of different nationalities were arguing about the proportion of the population that could board a starship in the end, which could even lead to new conflicts.

Other than that, although the ‘Battle of Superpowers’ was over, the Radiation Clan and the modified people who used to live on the ground were not entirely wiped out. The muscular guys who boasted a lot of ‘superpowers’ were the best workers and warriors. They would be of great help to the plan of the entire civilization to march into the universe. But at the same time, they were also full of resentment and hatred for the underground people, or rather, those in power, and considered the Earth Council to be their biggest enemy. They did not hesitate to perish together with the underground people and sabotage the plan of the Earth Council’s starship production. “You bastards have made a mess of the planet and are planning to run away now, but you are leaving us here to die? Let’s die together!”

It was the cry of many radiationers and modified people at that time.

How to deal with the ultranationalists, the radiationers, and the modified people who were hiding in the darkness, was a headache for the highest authorities of the Earth Council, especially the latter. A hundred super experts could be crushed by a hundred heavy tanks easily in a head-on battle, but when a hundred of them snuck into various shelters and military bases in disguise, the damage that they could cause was too much for even ten thousand tanks to stop.

Although the spiritual war was over, the lucky survivors of the war had found the right way to utilize their abilities.

They were corroding the perilous escape plan like fatal viruses.

Politically speaking, Li Yao had always advocated for an open and aboveboard democratic and republican system. He believed that power absolutely could not be concentrated on a single person. It had to be supervised by the entire nation.

He had foiled the plots of the ambitious schemers who claimed that they were ‘saving the civilization’ countless times.

However, faced with the apocalyptic crisis of the post-Wasteland Era, his belief was clouded without him knowing. He had to admit that democracy could not save the planet that was riddled with holes and deformed. Only an iron-willed man could bring new life to the entire civilization.

“Maybe Boss Bai, Li Jialing, Long Yangjun, and even Ding Lingdang are more suitable for such tests than I am.

Li Yao laughed at himself.

There was another person who was perhaps more suitable for such a test than anybody else – Jin Tuyi, who was already dead.

If Jin Tuyi had been the leader of the Earth at that time, how would he have handled such a chaotic situation?

Li Yao recalled everything about Jin Tuyi in his head. He retrieved every piece of information about Jin Tuyi from the depths of his brain and condensed them together with his amazing computational ability.

He simulated his logical way of thinking into Jin Tuyi and resolved the problem at hand in Jin Tuyi’s way.

Chapter 3100: Women And Children First

“If it is Jin Tuyi…”

Dark gold waves were gradually spreading out of Li Yao’s soul, which looked like hidden, sharp demonic energy.

At first, he chose to bow and scrape to the magnates and magnates of the old days so that they would believe that he was their most loyal spokesperson and lackey. Also, in the escape plan in a hundred years, he would definitely fight for the most positions for their families and forces, which would be supported by the magnates and the rich.

Then, he infiltrated the academic world and took control of several top research institutes. On one hand, the propaganda greatly reduced the difficulty of building starships and developing hibernation technology. On the other hand, he even invented several habitable planets dozens of lightyears away without any evidence. They were all worlds with abundant resources and abundant crystals and spiritual gas. In such a way, he got everyone’s support and forced a lot of projects that were destined to consume tremendous resources and were almost black holes.

He even arranged for people to bewitch the public and spread the message that, dozens of lightyears away, it would be everyone’s “Land of Hope, Land of Rebirth, Land of Eternal Glory”. As long as they could sleep comfortably for a few hundred years, they would be able to start a new happy life on a planet that was hundreds of times better than Earth, provided that all the citizens could grit their teeth and squeeze out every penny in their pockets as well as every drop of blood in their body for the sake of their plan to march into the universe. On the other hand, he had also raised a private army made of old soldiers, radiationers, and modified people in the darkness. He bared his sharp fangs at many of his opponents.

His private soldiers pretended to be the remnants of the superhumans who had been defeated in the ‘Battle of Superpowers’. They assassinated a lot of people who opposed him in the Earth Council. Then, they pretended to be the most extreme nationalists and ‘pure humanists’ and attacked the nests of the superhumans wantonly, raising the fire of hatred between the two parties. In such a way, the superhumans, the extreme nationalists, and all the opponents suffered heavy losses and further concentrated the power in their hands.

He had also secretly established a lot of state-of-the-art weapon research centers and transmitted the most advanced military technology to his private soldiers in order to improve his own combat ability and gradually corrupt the regular army.

Faced with the apocalypse, the leader of the Earth was like a bandit in a mountain of blades and a sea of flames, cruel and bloody.

And Li Yao knew that, under the advancement of history, there were even more unacceptable and inhumane details.

Thankfully, it had happened billions of years ago. Thankfully, it was just a test or a game. Thankfully, he did not have to deal with the vivid details and victims. Otherwise, his belief might have collapsed at this moment.

His ruthlessness and independence had finally paid off.

Stimulated by the apocalyptic crisis, the wisdom and courage of mankind were boosted by a hundredfold. Together with the discovery of spiritual energy and the emergence of superpowered Cultivators, the tanks of the civilization that had stagnated for decades finally started functioning again. The golden era turned into the platinum era. The technological boom continued unabated. The fields of astronomy, aeronautics, energy, materials science, human science, and so on experienced rapid breakthroughs. Almost every three to five years, groundbreaking, groundbreaking research was put into the field of application. Glittering cities of silver, vacuum tubes, anti-gravity devices, unmanned floating discs, remote sensing systems, and other technologies that represented ‘future’ and ‘hope’ entered every household. Like the mini-computers decades ago, they were everywhere and nothing unusual.

When there were only thirty years left in the countdown, the first large-scale immigration starship modified from an old carrier was finally built. It was successfully freed from the shackles of gravity and entered the orbit of the moon. The first batch of volunteers for the hibernation project woke up successfully after ten years of hibernation!

Everything seemed to be developing in the best direction.

But Li Yao’s heart was still in the air. Because he was very clear that sending tens of thousands of people into space was absolutely different from sending everyone on Earth into space. The difficulty was exponentially higher.

Right now, although Earth had been savaged by the third world war and the super battles, after decades of rest, it had regained the population of 2.5 billion.

It was an impossible task to send 2.5 billion people to an immigration ship thousands of lightyears away in the unknown sea of stars.

Even so, Li Yao still tried his best to save as many people as possible.

Among the billions of options, Li Yao keenly grasped an option named ‘Death Education’.

Among the countless technologies that were waiting to be developed, he chose a technology named ‘disciplined machine teaching’ which, together with ‘auto-driving’, became a key research project.

But this time, the two options did not proceed smoothly. Instead, two streaks of redness were emitting earsplitting beeps.

Li Yao was dazed, not knowing what was going on.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

“I’m sorry that you made the wrong choice. ‘Death education’ and ‘discipline machine teaching’ are not antidotes for our civilization.

The voice echoed inside his brain. “You’ve lost your first opportunity. But before you propose a reasonable proposal, I’m interested to know why you are doing this. What is your plan?”

Had he made the wrong choice?

He had lost one of the three opportunities. In other words, he had lost one of the three ‘lives’.

Li Yao took a deep breath and reminded himself not to panic. He had built the first immigration starship and only lost his first ‘life’ at this point. It should be a performance beyond the average standard. He still had two chances. Don’t be hasty. Take it slow.

“Based on the resources and the progress of my research, it is impossible for me to run away with everybody in the next thirty years.

Li Yao said calmly, “No matter how hard we try, the best we can do is run away with three to five hundred million people on board. In other words, we can only save one out of five.

“Yes.”

The voice said, “Your judgement is correct and even overly optimistic. Only one out of five can be saved. It is indeed the biggest problem for our civilization. The human heart is even more terrifying than the scourge of meteorites and black holes. How are you going to solve the problem?”

“I can’t. I am just a tiny human being and not an omnipotent god.

Li Yao smiled bitterly. “I can only try to think of the fairest and most reasonable way to decide who is qualified to board the immigration starship in the end.

“In my opinion, ‘the old, the weak, the women, and the children’ are the priority. It is a natural law. Considering the uniqueness of saving a civilization, the old and the weak, who don’t have much time left, cannot be the priority. Therefore, women and children are the most qualified to board the immigration starships. In a ratio of five to one, we might be able to save all the women and children.

“Fair enough.”

The voice said, “The women and the children are the hope of a civilization. The males are just consumables and reproductive tools. One male is enough to impregnate a hundred females and give birth to an entire tribe. Conversely, a hundred males and one female can only give birth to a few offspring no matter how hard they try. It is not enough to maintain the functioning of a civilization.

“Therefore, the immigration starship does not need to carry too many men. Theoretically speaking, it is. The question is, how did you do it?”

“Therefore, I chose the ‘Death Education’ strategy. Li Yao explained, “It is not a piece of technology. On the contrary, it is more like a philosophy. It teaches everyone how to face death, how to think about the relationship between an individual and a civilization, and how to look at their short life and the long journey of a civilization. In the end, I hope that people can look at their own life more calmly and peacefully, and I hope that they can be more willing to sacrifice themselves and pin all their hopes on the next generation.

“In the end, when the migration fleet sets off, all the children, pregnant women, and young ladies will be allowed to board the starship according to their age in the census. If there are empty cabins, they will line up according to their age. “I see.”

The voice said calmly, “That is to say, in your ideal state, the main passengers of the immigration fleet are women and children. Then, how are they going to steer the starship and colonize it when they reach their destination?”

“Sailing in space is not as complicated as it seems most of the time. Li Yao replied, “99.99% of the space zones in the universe are vacuum where there is nothing. It is much more peaceful than sailing in the ocean. In the worst-case scenario, it doesn’t matter who is controlling it.

“Since 99% of the passengers are hibernating, does it matter whether they are children or adults?

“Besides, in a situation where all kinds of cutting-edge weapons are popular and spiritual energy and crystals can significantly change the human body, I don’t think that women are necessarily weaker than men and cannot play the roles of soldiers and scientists. For example, I know a woman whose toughness… you won’t understand even if I tell you. “Besides, I’ve decided to devote a lot of resources to the development of ‘automatic education robots’, or fully-automatic education systems. When the children wake up from their long hibernation, they will be able to learn how to steer a starship and save the civilization with the help of supercomputers and fully-automatic robots.

“The stronger a young man is, the stronger his civilization will be. Although I know that there are still a lot of loopholes in the plan and even a lot of wishful thinking, I sincerely hope that the young men can start a new era of the Primeval Civilization—the civilization of Earth. I wonder, where have I gone wrong?”
